# Atlas — Backlog

**Single source of truth for open and deferred work.** Priority-ordered. One line per item with a link to its issue/PR where one exists.

> 🗂️ **Completed & cancelled items have moved to [`BACKLOG_ARCHIVE.md`](./BACKLOG_ARCHIVE.md)** (2026-06-29). This file now tracks only **open / in-progress / deferred** work. A handful of shipped (✅) items remain here because they still carry an open follow-up sub-note.

Seeded from the open GitHub issues, the not-yet-done items in [`FIX_PLAN.md`](./FIX_PLAN.md), the still-live findings in [`AUDIT.md`](./AUDIT.md) (the 2026-06-14 read-only code audit), and owner decisions. See the "Backlog discipline" section in [`CLAUDE.md`](./CLAUDE.md) for how to keep this current.

### Deferred item tags

Each deferred item should carry one of these tags so future agents know the *risk* of the PR it warrants:

- `[trust-critical]` — could silently corrupt data, produce wrong numbers, or break the preview→approve→write path.
- `[correctness]` — wrong behavior visible to user but no data corruption risk.
- `[polish]` — wording, ordering, or UX quality. No behavior change.
- `[housekeeping]` — dead code, duplicate logic, test gaps, doc cleanup.

> **Model:** the builder runs on **Opus 4.8 for all work** (owner standing instruction — see `CLAUDE.md`). The per-item "Sonnet 4.6 / Opus 4.8" suggestions still scattered through older items below are **superseded** — read them as risk hints only, not model selection.

   - ✅ **`[trust-critical]` PARSER BUG — silent set MISATTRIBUTION — FIXED 2026-07-03** (same day it was found; the `todo` reproducers in `test/parserFuzz.test.js` are now HARD assertions plus two new same-line guards). Root cause was two stacked holes in `parseLogSets`: (1) the G1 trailing guard required EVERY word of an inter-set run to be name-like, so one notation/alias word ("press" is a contextual-alias word, "x3") poisoned the run and let "… x3 Zzqx Press 185 5/2" absorb silently — now `some(isNameWord)`, strictly more conservative; (2) no LEADING guard existed — slash sets BEFORE the matched name ("Zzqx Press 185 5/2 Bench 200 5/2") were vacuumed into the found lift — now the G1 leading analogue dead-ends them with `unattributable_trailing_sets`, which also routes newline pastes into the existing multi-line rescue where each line resolves correctly (the unknown name becomes its own flagged freeform entry; the #820 check-name chip covers the surface). All previously-clean grammars verified byte-identical (single/multi-set/prose-noise/2-line carry/known-multi); suite 4515/4515; browser sweep clean. Original finding record follows for history: an unknown exercise name on a set-bearing line can have its sets absorbed by a DIFFERENT, known lift — worse than dropping, it corrupts the wrong lift's history at save. Repro A (collapse path): `"Zzqx Press 185 5/2 - solid\nBench 200 5/2"` → log_sets for Bench Press with BOTH sets (185 under Bench), zero warnings — the newline collapse treats the unknown leading name as noise and only one KNOWN exercise exists, so the multi-exercise dead-end (which would trigger the line-by-line rescue) never fires. Repro B (rescue path): `"Leg Press …\nRDL 225 5/2 x3\nZzqx Press 185 5/2 - solid"` → RDL grows a phantom 185 set via the rescue loop's carry-forward (parseLogSets with activeExercise attaches the line's sets to the carried lift when the line's own name fails). Fix direction: a set-bearing line with its own leading name must never merge into a different exercise — unresolvable names go to `unresolved` (rescue) and the collapse path must dead-end into the rescue when an unknown leading name precedes a known one. High-risk `services/workoutTextParser.js` surgery — do it as a focused fresh-session PR with G1 regression coverage, not as a tail-of-session patch.

   - **C3 evidence log — shadow router LIVE (owner flipped `ATLAS_INTENT_ROUTER=shadow` on Render, 2026-07-03)** `[correctness / product-evidence]`: first live classification (from `/api/debug/intent-shadow`): *"My legs are fried right now I think I'll skip single leg press and leg extension"* → `modify_workout`, confidence 0.9, `exclude_exercises` extracted, 745ms off-path. **The live path fumbled the same message**: it fell through to the Gemini chat, which pushed back on the fatigue claim ("your logs actually look recovered") and never removed the exercises — the session pin kept "next: Leg Extension" for the rest of the workout. Root cause: dropping a planned exercise currently happens only if the chat voice CHOOSES to emit `PROPOSE_PLAN_EDIT: remove_exercises` (`services/coach.js` chat prompt); no deterministic skip lane exists for stated intent. This is the canonical C3 case — `modify_workout` should route deterministically to the plan-mutation lane. **Promotion stays owner/evidence-gated**: keep collecting shadow entries; when the log shows consistent accuracy, propose the C3 wiring as its own owner-visible decision.
   - ✅ **C3 shadow COVERAGE gap — most typed messages were never observed — FIXED (chokepoint-observe PR)** `[product-evidence]`: owner ran many shadow sessions, `/api/debug/intent-shadow` showed only 2 entries. Root cause: `observeChatMessage` fired at ONE place — server-side inside `/api/coach/chat` — but the composer routes typed text through ~8 deterministic lanes that each return before any chat call (set logs → parse/preview, session/artifact asks → local, log-it → compile, **plan mutations + identity corrections → client-only, no network at all**, modality → `/api/log-modality`), and even chat-reaching messages hit `/api/coach/ask` (SME) first and return before `/api/coach/chat` when answered. So the log only ever saw the residual free-form questions ("What's next"). Fix: observation moved to the ONE composer chokepoint — a fire-and-forget `observeComposerText` at the top of the `logger-form` submit handler POSTs every free-text submission to a new auth-gated, observe-only `POST /api/debug/intent-observe` (declared in `config/routes.js`), which forwards to `observeChatMessage`; the redundant `/api/coach/chat` call was removed (single observation point, no double-count). Still observe-only, off the reply/write path, server no-ops when the flag is off. Programmatic canned `routeMessageToCoach` calls (pattern-board/nav) are intentionally not observed (not free-text typed). Shell v103→v104. Review #838 fix: the observe POST uses a BARE authenticated fetch (not `api()`) so a dropped observation never lands in the `atlasLastError`/request-history that bug reports capture — observation stays truly invisible. **Known-harmless limitation (#838 review note):** a few flows re-dispatch a synthetic `submit` with the user's text still in the composer ("Log as new session", compile re-submit, manual-effort), so the same text can occasionally be observed twice — fine for diagnostics, and the blank-guard no-ops the empty-composer re-dispatches. (Persistence to a Sheet tab so review needs no debug JSON — SHIPPED next, see below.)

We're done."* classify as `clarify_intent` 0.3 because the IntentEnvelope vocabulary (`config/coaching/contracts/intent.vocabulary.json`, 13 intent types) has **no session-lifecycle / finish-session / save / confirmation-card intent**. (Note: *"Where's the session confirmation card?"* is a UI-help question, not a finish intent — `clarify_intent` is defensible for it; the real gap is only the two "done" phrases.) **Decide: add a `finish_session`/`session_lifecycle` intent (and a `help`/`app_question` intent for UI questions?), or leave these as `clarify_intent`.**
     - **Blocker 2 — "It's face pulls next not back squats" → `ok:false` — FIXED:** root cause was a **timeout** (`ms:4004`, at the old 4000ms cap), not a misclassification — the near-identical *"No that was face pulls not back squats"* classified fine (`substitute_exercise` 0.9). Raised `DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` 4000→6000 (shadow runs fire-and-forget off the reply path, so the budget is invisible to the lifter), added an in-prompt exemplar routing "not Y, X instead / it's X next not Y" plan corrections to `substitute_exercise` with `target_lift`+`exclude_exercises`, and a full-path regression fixture. (Distinct from the separate finding that a PAST-tense *"that was X not Y"* is an identity correction, not a substitution — the router has no correction intent; captured under the C3 evidence log for the promotion decision, not fixed here.)
     - **Blocker 3 — `log_intent` dropped `sets/reps/weight_kg/rir` — EXPECTED, documented:** those are not constraint-vocabulary keys; the IntentEnvelope has no set-detail keys by design (the deterministic slash-parser owns numbers, the router only names the intent), so the model's hallucinated set keys are correctly dropped+recorded. Added a passing regression fixture pinning the behavior + a prompt/gloss note telling the model not to emit set details.
   - ✅ **Live find (owner session 2026-07-03): stated skip fell to the chat LLM — FIXED same day** `[correctness / trust]` — *"My legs are fried right now I think I'll skip single leg press and leg extensions"* never reached the deterministic plan-mutation lane: (1) `classifyMutationIntent` was start-anchored, so a leading REASON clause defeated every pattern; (2) `resolvePlanTargets` couldn't bridge "single leg press" → "Single-Leg Seated Leg Press" (hyphen + extra word beat the substring tiers). Fix (`public/planMutationIntent.js`): a negation- and interrogative-guarded reason-clause fallback (first-person intent marker + mutation verb → re-run the SAME anchored grammar on the extracted clause) and a ≥2-word token-subset match tier. Stated intent now mutates the plan deterministically — pin, composer, and next-up move on; the chat keeps everything that isn't a clear mutation. (The same message is C3 evidence entry #1 — the deterministic lane now covers this case regardless of the router's future.)
   - ✅ **Owner directive (2026-07-03, live screenshots): plans render STACKED, never as prose lines** — the chat's plan reply rendered "RDL — 230 × 8 reps × 3 sets @ RIR 2" linear prose; the owner wants the Coach's-Pick stacked block (bold name, one set per line) everywhere a plan renders. Shipped: (1) applied chat plan edits (`replace_plan`/`add_exercises`) render `appendWorkoutPlan` from the edit's own deterministic numbers under the reply; (2) the chat prompt instructs the model NOT to enumerate the plan in prose (the app owns the render); (3) `looksLikeSessionRequest` gained suggest/recommend phrasings ("what workout would you suggest for today", "recommend me a session") so those asks land on the canonical stacked pick in the first place.
   - ✅ **Live bug (owner 2026-07-03, 11:23): an alias log against a CHAT-created plan never advanced the plan — FIXED** `[correctness / trust]` — logged "Rdl 240 8/3 ×3"; the handoff said "next up: Romanian Deadlift" and the composer stayed pointed at it. Three stacked gaps: applied `PROPOSE_PLAN_EDIT` plans carry names only (no lift codes) so the code bridge was one-sided; the exercise datalist held canonical names only (no variants, so "RDL" resolved no code client-side); and the enrichment dry-run is best-effort (null on a slow network). Fix (`public/app.js`): the datalist now includes catalog VARIANTS (value=variant, label=code, deduped) so `liftCodeFromCatalog('RDL')`→`RDL01`; `resolveCompletedIdentity` bridges codes TWO-SIDED (entry code falls back to the catalog by canonical/name) and gains the ≥2-word word-subset tier (mirrors `planMutationIntent`). Bonus: the logger typeahead now suggests variants.
   - **Follow-up — `[polish]` #830 review note: add_exercises stacked block shows already-present exercises** — `applyProposedPlanEdit` skips duplicates when adding, but the stacked confirmation block renders ALL of `edit.exercises`, so an exercise the model "adds" that was already in the plan re-renders as if fresh (it is in the plan — redundant, never false). Filter the block to the exercises actually added if it bothers in practice.
   - **Follow-up — `[polish]` #829/#831 review note: word-subset tie-break for same-family duplicate slots** — BOTH subset-tier call sites inherit the ambiguity: `planMutationIntent.resolvePlanTargets` matches every superset slot (half-mitigated by `tryApplyPlanMutation`'s single-phrase `slice(0,1)`), and `app.js resolveCompletedIdentity`'s word-subset tier returns the FIRST matching slot in plan order ("incline press" against Incline Barbell + Incline Dumbbell Press marks the first). Decide one tie-break (e.g. prefer the current/first-pending slot, or refuse and ask) and add a fuzz cell covering both sites if same-family duplicate slots become common.

An engaged-but-unlogged plan no longer auto-reactivates guided mode (it's one tap to re-open the pick); sessions WITH logged sets resume exactly as before (banner + discard intact). Today's read: {label}."` (from `todays_read.recommended_label`) and a facts line = `buildConsistencyText` + `buildPatternBriefing` (freshest/overdue patterns, most-recovered/overdue first, up to two named, `''` when none). `coach-conversation.js`'s `renderCoachOpening` is now an `atlas:glance-ready` listener that upgrades `#coach-opening` and reveals the new `#coach-facts` line — no second network call, LLM-free, guarded on the hero still being visible. Degrades to the unchanged default tagline + guide when nothing meaningful arrives (no key/offline/cold-start → no dispatch). Shell v101→v102. Tests: opener redesign (greeting still synchronous + fetch-free; briefing renders app.js strings, no fetch/LLM; app.js headline/facts/degradation; `buildPatternBriefing` golden). ## Atlas Flight Recorder — replayable real-session transcript (owner-approved design 2026-07-05)

Design spec: [`docs/FLIGHT_RECORDER_SPEC.md`](./docs/FLIGHT_RECORDER_SPEC.md). An **optional, feature-flagged (`ATLAS_FLIGHT_RECORDER=1`, default OFF), append-only** logging system that records the **user-visible app experience + decision trail** during real sessions, so a developer can **replay** what happened instead of debugging by screenshots and pasted JSON. It answers: *"what did the user do, what did Atlas think, what did Atlas decide, and what exactly did Atlas show?"*. Owner/debug telemetry — NOT training data: writes only to a new optional `Flight_Recorder` tab, never `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/`Modality_Log`, no `write_id`, never routes through the preview→approve→write trust loop. Best-effort + TOTAL (a recorder failure can never surface a user error, block the UI, retry-storm, or interrupt logging). Owner decisions (2026-07-05): JSON-in-cells MVP schema; batching = flush at 25 events / every 10s / immediately on error·bug_marker·pagehide; full `correlation_id` excluded from initial scope (→ FR5).

Build as tiny PRs, one concern each:
- ✅ **PR-FR1 — schema + contract + pure core (no writes)** `[housekeeping]` — **shipped (PR #856).** `Flight_Recorder` added to `optionalSheetTabs` (`config/sheetContract.js`); `flightRecorderColumns` (18-col JSON-in-cells contract) added to `config/columns.js`; new pure `services/flightRecorder.js` (`buildFlightRow`/`buildFlightRows`, capped newest-first in-memory ring, `redactFlightEvent` reusing `services/bugReport.js`'s `redactBugPayload`, flag gate default-OFF, event taxonomy). **UNWIRED** — nothing appends to Sheets, opens a route, or touches any runtime path yet. Tests: `test/flightRecorder.test.js` (row/contract parity, secret redaction + truncation, blank/junk handling, ring cap, flag gate, TOTAL on hostile input). No write-path/trust-loop/proof-field/parser change.
- ✅ **PR-FR2 — server-side API-flow recording + read route** `[correctness]` — **shipped (PR #856).** `recordApiFlow` (`services/flightRecorder.js`) builds one `api_response` event per served request, rings it, and best-effort appends one row via the standard `sheets.appendRows` — so it targets the **server's own `GOOGLE_SHEETS_ID`** (sandbox server → sandbox `Flight_Recorder`; production → production; no second sheet target). A thin **observe-only middleware** in `index.js` (flag-gated, skips `/api/flight/*`, records on `res.on('finish')`, never reads/mutates the response, never blocks) captures **both real app traffic and the simulation harness** (an HTTP client driving the server). `GET /api/flight/recent` serves the in-memory ring. **Isolation:** the sim harness marks requests `x-atlas-simulation: 1`, and `recordApiFlow` never persists sim-marked traffic to a non-sandbox sheet (ring-only) — so a sim can never write into the production `Flight_Recorder`, on top of sim write-mode already refusing non-sandbox servers. **`request_summary` is SHAPE-only** (top-level keys + size, never field values), so workout content never lands in the debug tab (owner decision 2026-07-05, honoring the "prefer summaries for large JSON" guardrail; from review #856 feedback). Proofs: `test/flightRecorderApiFlow.test.js` (flag-off=zero writes; sim→non-sandbox=no write / sim→sandbox=write / real→any=write; redaction; TOTAL) + `test/api-smoke.test.js` end-to-end (flag gate, `Flight_Recorder`-only, sim isolation) + a harness sim-header proof. No `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/trust-tab write, no proof-field/trust-loop/parser change; flag default-OFF → byte-identical.
  - Deferred `[housekeeping]`: server-side **batching** (currently one best-effort append per API request, consistent with the shadow-lane volume and fine for a debug window; batch if a high-traffic observation window makes the append count matter).
- ✅ **PR-FR3 — frontend capture + batching + client ingest** `[correctness]` — **shipped (this PR).** New self-contained `public/flightRecorder.js` (UMD, never throws) that attaches its OWN listeners — capture-phase clicks (`user_action`), composer `#logger-form` submit (`user_input`), the existing `atlas:*` CustomEvents (`preview-ready`/`chat-message`/`set-logged`/`plan-mutated`/… → `card_rendered`/`coach_message_rendered`/`session_state_changed`), `pagehide`/`visibilitychange` — and reads the DOM for `ui_snapshot`/`session_state`, so it needs **NO edit to the restricted `public/app.js`** (only the standard shell-version bump v107→v108 + SW precache). It self-activates ONLY when `GET /api/flight/recent` reports the flag on (else fully inert); batched flush (25 events / 10s / on error·bug_marker / `pagehide` via `fetch` keepalive so the auth header survives). `POST /api/flight/ingest` (writeCapable, rate-limited) → `recordClientBatch` appends the whole batch in ONE best-effort call to `Flight_Recorder` (never a workout/trust tab), **202 no-op when the flag is off**, same sim→non-sandbox isolation guard. Proofs: `test/flightRecorderClient.test.js` + `test/api-smoke.test.js` ingest end-to-end. Full suite green. No trust-tab write, no proof-field/trust-loop/parser change (the only `public/app.js` touch is the version const). **Review #857 fixes:** `/api/flight/ingest` gets its OWN `flight_ingest` rate-limiter bucket (generous default) so best-effort telemetry can NEVER draw from the trust-write budget and 429 a real set-log/undo; the client also captures JS `error`/`unhandledrejection` events (makes the taxonomy's `error` type + the immediate-flush branch live); `recordClientBatch` catch reports the real flag state.
  - **Deferred `[correctness]` — shadow-linkage enrichment (`_flight.shadow`)**: additive response-envelope block (Brain/Intent created/route/count) the client stamps onto its event; deferred from FR3 to keep that slice to capture + ingest. Small follow-up.
- ✅ **Live-validation follow-up — API-event session linkage** `[correctness]` — **shipped (this PR).** Server-side `api_response` events had blank `flight_session_id`/`seq`/`device_id`, making the transcript hard to replay. Fix: the client sends `x-atlas-flight-session`/`x-atlas-device-id` on every app `/api` call (`flightRecorder.requestHeaders()` spread into `app.js` `api()` — `{}` and byte-identical when the recorder is off), the FR2 middleware already reads them, and `recordApiFlow` derives a server-side per-session monotonic `seq` (blank when unlinked; explicit seq still wins). Scoped to Flight Recorder API-event linkage only — **not** the FR5 Brain_Shadow/Intent_Shadow `correlation_id`. Test-first (`test/flightRecorderApiFlow.test.js` seq/linkage + `test/flightRecorderClient.test.js` requestHeaders/app.js-wiring). Shell v109→v110. No write-path/proof-field/trust-loop/parser change; best-effort preserved.
- ✅ **PR-FR4 — Settings → Debug UX** `[polish]` — **shipped (this PR).** A "Flight Recorder (debug)" card in Settings (`public/index.html`) wired entirely from `public/flightRecorder.js` (runtime exports `markIssue`/`getSessionId` + `wireDebugUi`; **no `app.js` handler edits** — only the shell-version bump v108→v109 + SW cache): shows enabled state + current `flight_session_id`, **Refresh** renders the last-20 events from `GET /api/flight/recent`, **Copy transcript** puts the ring JSON on the clipboard, **Mark issue** drops a `bug_marker` event (flushed promptly). Degrades gracefully when the flag is off. Proofs: `test/flightRecorderClient.test.js` (markIssue/getSessionId inert-when-off + TOTAL; index.html controls present; client wires the buttons + emits `bug_marker`) + the shell-cache guardrail (v109). Full suite 4692/4692. No trust-tab/proof-field/trust-loop/parser change.
- **PR-FR5 (owner-gated) — full `correlation_id`** `[trust-critical]`: mint a per-request id and append it as a trailing column to `Brain_Shadow` + `Intent_Shadow` (append-only schema change → owner approval) so Flight_Recorder ↔ shadow tabs join on one exact key. Deferred by owner (2026-07-05).
- ✅ **Flight-review evidence extractor (READ-ONLY)** `[housekeeping]` — **shipped.** New `scripts/flight-review.js`: a read-only exporter that pulls `Flight_Recorder` / `Brain_Shadow` / `Intent_Shadow` / `Log_Cleaned` / `Bug_Reports`, groups by `flight_session_id`, correlates the shadow lanes by captured_at time-window and workout rows by session_id (date fallback), and writes `outputs/flight-review/<timestamp>.{json,md}` (gitignored). Flags six anomaly classes (repeated idle coach messages, 4xx/5xx, missing session ids, repeated completed exercises, session-confirm mismatch, coach-not-answering) as triage candidates with evidence. Uses the Sheets **read-only** scope (mirrors `scripts/export-sheets-backup.js`) — never writes, never touches the trust path, changes no runtime behavior. Offline `--from-dir=<backup>` mode + `--sheet`/`--window-mins` flags. Tests: `test/flightReview.test.js` (parser drift, grouping, correlation, each detector, end-to-end render). **Follow-ups (deferred, NOT built — evidence/planning only per task scope):** (1) MVP shadow correlation is time-window heuristic (coarse; a precise join waits on **PR-FR5** `correlation_id`); (2) `Log_Cleaned` date-window fallback over-attach — **partially addressed (PR #868):** `correlateLogs` now prefers `session_id` matches and only engages the same-day date-window fallback when NO session_id resolved, and `correlateByTime` tags shadow lanes `route+time` vs `time`. **Tradeoff noted (review #868):** once any session_id resolves, same-day rows with a blank/absent session_id are now dropped rather than surfaced as weaker `date_window` context — fine for owner-facing evidence, but if a future session-boundary edge case wants those low-confidence rows back, revisit here; a precise correlation_id join still waits on **PR-FR5**; (3) anomaly heuristics read coach text from `ui_snapshot_json`/`decision_summary_json` candidate keys — revisit if the recorder's snapshot field names change.
- **Flight-test triage 2026-07-06 (independent telemetry review)** — ran the extractor against the live workbook's latest cycle (single ~20-min session, 8 `bug_marker`s). **Shipped fixes:** "over head press" spacing alias (PR #869); unknown-lift log survives conversational filler before its sets — "Curls I did 35 12/2 x3 actually" (PR #870); a feedback opening line no longer drops the following log line — "You missed curls" (PR #871). **Deferred / uncertain (NOT built — need owner or more scope):**
  - `[correctness]` **Feedback line → coach routing**: after PR #871, an opening feedback line ("weight you suggested is too light") is surfaced as an `unresolved` line while the log is recovered. Routing that feedback to the coach as a modify/clarify signal (so Atlas *responds* to it, not just logs past it) is a larger interaction concern — build as its own PR.
  - `[correctness]` **"I'll skip RDLs today" not honored**: a natural-language skip mixed with a log ("I'll skip RDLs today\n\nBench …") parses the Bench fine but drops the skip into `unresolved_lines`; the plan still lists RDL as pending (marker "Alta's got tripped up"). Honoring an inline skip = a plan-mutation intent (feature), not a tiny parser fix.
  - **coaching-philosophy → owner-reserved** (not code bugs): OHP "only two working sets" (marker seq18 — a *question*; engine prescribed 1 warm-up + 2 working by design; at most a transparency gap); coach commentary appearing on a plain set log (marker "Not coaches note here again").
  - `[correctness]` **recheck after #870/#871**: the session recap listed already-logged lifts as "still on your plan" and the curls coaching pulled a ~105–116 lb working range (Overhead Press's, not curls') — both partly downstream of the two parse drops now fixed; re-verify on the next live cycle before opening a PR. Also low-pri: `log-modality` 422 on "You missed curls" (likely correct validation).

**Recommendation (owner-approved):** build the FR1–FR3 vertical slice before further simulator expansion — the simulator can't reproduce real-device UI state the owner is currently screenshotting.

Decide when their capability areas are next touched; do not wire speculatively.

## Bug-report tooling

- ✅ **Recovery/deload session still nudges "add load"** `[correctness]` (BUG-20260629-034034) — **shipped (this PR).** A Coach's Pick Recovery/Pump session replied "Too much left in the tank. Bump coming" — telling the lifter to add load on a deliberate deload day. **Root (refined during the fix):** the visible line is the *deterministic* set-voice (`effortNote`/`renderSetVoice` from `services/setEffortCopy.js`/`coachVoiceRenderer.js`), driven by `analyzeSetSequence`'s `bump` progression verdict — computed **blind to the recovery/deload objective** and rendered server-side regardless of whether Gemini is up. (Slice 6's suppression only covered the client-side LLM-down templated path.) **Fix (source-level, both paths):** (1) new pure `suppressBumpForRecovery(analysis, recoveryActive)` (`services/setEffortSignals.js`) neutralizes ONLY the under-dose `bump` verdict (and its reason code/signal) when a recovery objective is active — `block`/`caution` reads are untouched; wired into `computeSetEffortExtras` (`index.js`) by computing the recovery read UP FRONT (deload active OR a `deload`/`recovery_reload` selection) and gating the analysis before any voice renders, so both `effort_note` and `voiceBase` go quiet on the bump. `classifySeverity` only derives `bump` from the analysis verdict (never from `effort_verdict`), so neutralizing the verdict fully suppresses the deterministic add-load voice. (2) LLM prompt (`buildCoachSystemPrompt`, `services/coach.js`): the `recovery_advisory` rule now **overrides** the `effort_verdict` add-weight steer (same strength as the active-deload rule), so the prose can't independently tell the lifter to add load when the set read easy. (3) Review follow-up — closed the last LLM-path steer: `set_grade` (the Stimulus Governor) graded a high-RIR recovery set as `+load` ("room to add stimulus"); new `holdStimulusForRecovery` downgrades a `+load`/`+reps` grade to `hold` when recovery is active, so no fact forwarded to the model invites adding load. Correctness fix, not a coaching-philosophy change. Tests: 3 new in `test/setEffortSignals.test.js` (bump neutralized on recovery; untouched off-recovery; block/caution never touched) + updated `test/coach.test.js` recovery_advisory prompt assertion. No write-path / trust-loop / proof-field / schema / parser change.

## QA campaign — 2026-06-26 live gym + composer playtest (B1–B10)

Ordered remediation of the findings in [`docs/UX_PLAYTEST_2026-06-26_LIVE_GYM_AND_COMPOSER_SESSION.md`](./docs/UX_PLAYTEST_2026-06-26_LIVE_GYM_AND_COMPOSER_SESSION.md) (the source-of-truth record; §2 = the B1–B10 findings, §5 = the follow-up PR split). One concern per PR, in priority order. Owner runs a model hold point between PRs.

- ✅ **B4 — Freestyle logging must not auto-guide ("next up")** `[correctness]` (this PR) — **Verdict: BROKEN → fixed.** Root cause: `handleSetLogged` in `public/coach-conversation.js` always fell back to `getNextExerciseInPlan()` (→ `/api/plan/today`) when `detail.nextPlanned` was null, even during freestyle logging with an empty `plannedOrder`. Fix: gate the fallback on `hasEngagedPlan = (detail.plannedOrder || []).length > 0` — freestyle (empty `plannedOrder`) now yields `nextEx = null` immediately, so no "Moving on — next up: X" appears. Guided sessions (engaged `activePlannedSession` or `coachSuggestionEngaged`) still use the fallback when needed. The `inEngagedPlan` second-line guard and the duplicate-resurrection guard both remain. Tests: `test/freestyleNextUp.test.js` (5 structural cases) + updated assertion in `test/unit.test.js`.
  - **Deferred — `compactPrescription` double-normalization drops weight/reps for `activePlannedSession` entries** `[polish]`: `nextUpPlaceholderFromPlan` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) always returns `null` for entries from `activePlannedSession` because `compactPrescription` calls `normalizePlanExercise` on an **already-normalized** entry. Normalized entries carry `name`/`liftCode`/`weight`/`reps`/`sets`/`rir` but not `exercise`/`target_weight`/`target_reps` (the raw intent fields), so the second `normalizePlanExercise` call resolves `name='LPD01'` (from `liftCode`) and `weight=null`/`reps=null` → `compactPrescription` returns `null`. The engaged-session composer placeholder falls through to `formatNextPlaceholder` via `/api/plan/today` (expanded per-set format), or bare name if that call misses. Fix: either gate `compactPrescription` to skip the `normalizePlanExercise` call when the input is already normalized (has a `name`/`weight`/`reps` shape), or read `activePlannedSession` entries directly in `nextUpPlaceholderFromPlan`. No trust-loop / write-path / parser change — display only. File when the owner wants the compact `xN` placeholder in guided sessions (e.g. "Lat Pulldown 170 8/2 x3" vs the current expanded "Lat Pulldown 170 8/2 8/2 8/2").
---

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner live re-test (recovery-session scold, 2nd recurrence of BUG-20260629-204817)

- ✅ **[correctness] Recovery/Pump session scolds the prescribed light loads — "below your working range / add weight / Room to progress"** — **shipped (this PR).** Owner screenshots 07-02: a Recovery/Pump Coach's Pick prescribed 55×15@RIR4 / 90×12@RIR5; logging those exact sets produced "That was a light session… below your working range of 60–70… get back in the groove", "You're right on track to add weight next session", and a "Next time: Room to progress — move to 60 × 15" card. **Two root gaps left by the -204817 fix:** (1) `GET /api/recommend/next/:liftCode` (`index.js`) never read the `?intentId` query param the client has sent since that fix — so `recommendNextSet`'s recovery branch and recovery target-RIR were dead in production (normal-day target RIR 3 → RIR-5 sets read `far_easy` → add-load card + polluted `rec` facts fed to the coach LLM). (2) The prescribed intent never reached the LLM facts — `sanitizeFacts` (`services/coach.js`) had no field for it, and the prompt's recovery override keyed only off an active deload / convergence advisory, so the note worded `under_shot`/`far_easy` as a scold. **Fix:** route passes a whitelisted `intentId` (`recovery_pump`/`deload_reset` only — other intents byte-identical) into `recommendNextSet`; `sanitizeFacts` forwards `session_intent` (same whitelist) and `buildCoachSystemPrompt` frames a recovery session's light/high-RIR work as ON-PLAN by design (overrides the add-weight steer, forbids "get back in the groove"-style scolds). Read-only paths — no write/trust-loop/proof-field/schema/parser change. Tests: route control/recovery/junk-intent smoke (`test/api-smoke.test.js`), `session_intent` whitelist + prompt rule (`test/coach.test.js`).
  - [x] **Owner live re-test — PASSED 2026-07-02 10:58** (post-merge #788): owner ran the same Recovery/Pump session shape. OHP 90×12@RIR5 → "Dialled in — that landed right on target" + "Next time: On target — keep 90 and chase 13 reps next set" (load held); Seated Row 145×13@RIR4 → on target. No "add weight" / "below your range" scold, no load bump. Lane closed.
  - **Deferred — intent-aware target RIR for NON-recovery intents at the route** `[correctness]`: the `?intentId` passthrough is deliberately whitelisted to the two recovery objectives (smallest safe fix for the live bug). `recommendedTargetRir` supports every intent (`build_strength` → RIR 2, `build_muscle` accessory → 1, etc.), and the client already sends whatever intent is active — but the route still ignores non-recovery intents, so e.g. a strength session's effort verdict is judged against the general-health default target. Wiring the full intent set changes effort verdicts on normal days; do it as its own reviewed PR with fixture coverage per goal.
  - **Deferred — centralize the recovery-intent list** `[housekeeping]` (Codex review PR #788, non-blocking): `['recovery_pump', 'deload_reset']` is now duplicated across four sites — the `/api/recommend/next` route (`index.js`), `computeSetEffortExtras` (`index.js`), `RECOVERY_SESSION_INTENTS` (`services/coach.js`), and `recommendNextSet` (`services/analytics.js`) — coupled only by "keep in lockstep" comments. If a third recovery intent is ever added to `INTENT_TO_GOAL` (`services/rirPolicy.js`) without mirroring, the voices diverge silently. Export one shared constant (natural home: `services/rirPolicy.js`, e.g. derived from `INTENT_TO_GOAL` entries mapping to `recovery`) and consume it at all four sites in a future authorized touch of those files.

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner report (freestyle shorthand silently dropped)

- ✅ **[correctness] "Lat pull 175 10/2 x3" typed after Bench Press silently dropped instead of logged** — **shipped (this PR).** Owner screenshots 07-02 (freestyle session, no active plan): after logging Bench Press then Hammer Curl, "Lat pull 175 10/2 x3" got only "Noted — keep logging and say 'log it' when you're done" — no confirmation card, and the set was absent from the final session summary (2 exercises / 8 sets, missing Lat Pulldown entirely). **Root cause:** "lat pull" is a `CONTEXTUAL_ALIASES` entry for Lat Pulldown (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) that only auto-resolves when `context.activeExercise` already equals "Lat Pulldown" (cross-turn continuation); with a mismatched active exercise (Bench Press) it correctly falls to `needs_clarification` ("Did you mean Lat Pulldown?") but that response omitted the `partial.exercise` field the other `needs_clarification` branches in the same file set. The frontend's trust-gap guard (`public/app.js` — `err.parsedIntent === 'needs_clarification' && err.recognizedExercise`, added to stop exactly this class of silent drop) reads `parsed.partial.exercise` into `err.recognizedExercise`; with it null, the guard didn't fire and the typed set fell through to `routeMessageToCoach` → the generic numeric-text `chatFallback` reply, discarding the set with no trace. **Fix (`services/workoutTextParser.js`, additive-only):** the contextual-alias-mismatch `needs_clarification` return now carries `partial: { exercise, raw_name }` like its sibling branches, so the existing frontend guard surfaces "Did you mean Lat Pulldown? Use the full exercise name to be sure." as an actionable status message instead of a misleading "Noted" chat reply. No parser-grammar/alias-resolution change (contextual aliases still require the strong alias or a matching active exercise to auto-log — that scope stays as-is; see the related deferred shorthand items below), no write-path/schema/trust-loop change. Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (`contextual_alias_mismatch_carries_partial_exercise_so_it_is_not_silently_dropped`). Full suite green.
  - **Deferred — freestyle sessions still can't auto-log bare shorthand ("Lat pull") without the full name** `[polish]`: this PR closes the *silent-drop* trust gap (the lifter is now told to retype with the full name) but does not make "lat pull"/"incline" themselves loggable in a freestyle session with no matching active exercise — `rowsFromUnresolvedPlannedLead` only bridges shorthand when a **planned session** has that lift pending (see the "Still open" notes on the #480/#481 entries above). Promoting `lat pull` from a contextual to a strong alias (or a freestyle-aware bridge) is a slash-notation/parser-grammar contract change and stays owner-gated per `CLAUDE.md`.

## Bug remediation — 2026-07-02 owner live test (B5 date regression on the conversation surface)

- ✅ **[trust-critical] Vision model hallucinated the YEAR on a yearless screenshot header — 12 rows SAVED dated 2020-06-28 (live incident, 07-02 11:57, IMG_5453/5459). FIXED (plausibility-guard PR, 2026-07-02); sheet cleanup is owner-side.** The B5 fix correctly let the screenshot date win — which exposed that the vision model, given a yearless "June 28" watch header, returned `2020-06-28` as a "visible and unambiguous" date (June 28 is a Sunday in both 2020 and 2026 — it weekday-matched a wrong year). The card truthfully showed "Date from screenshot: 2020-06-28" (visibility worked) but a screenshot-sourced date had no correction affordance, and the owner saved: `Log_Cleaned` rows 1597–1608 (Bench 170×10@5 ×3, Hammer Curls 30×15@4 ×3, OHP 90×12@5 ×3, Seated Row 145×13@4 ×3) + the `20200628-AM-01` Effort row are dated six years in the past. **Shipped (this PR):** (1) deterministic server guard `isPlausibleScreenshotDate` (`index.js`) — a screenshot date >2 days ahead or >400 days back is REJECTED: never drives the save/session_id/effort row (gated at the resolution AND the effort-row builder), `date_source` falls back to `today_fallback`, and the response carries `screenshot_date_rejected` so the card words it honestly ("The screenshot's date reads 2020-06-28, which doesn't look right — saving as <today> unless you set the real date:") with the picker; (2) the same window in the client closeout check (`isConfidentScreenshotDate`, `public/app.js`); (3) vision prompt hardening (`services/vision.js`): the year must be printed — never inferred from the weekday — else `date: null`; (4) an ACCEPTED screenshot date is now also correctable on the card ("Wrong date? Fix it" reveals the shared picker row, which keeps the PR #795 session-id clear). Tests: 3 route-level guard tests (`test/api-smoke.test.js`, incl. manual-date-still-wins + rejected-date-never-reaches-the-effort-row; the pre-existing screenshot-date test de-time-bombed onto relative dates), prompt rule pinned (`test/vision.test.js`), 3 client introspection blocks (`test/unit.test.js`). Shell v81→v82. **OWNER DATA CLEANUP (production sheet, owner-side — hand edit):** in `Log_Cleaned`, rows 1597–1608: change column A `2020-06-28` → the session's real date (2026-06-28) and column B `20200628-AM-01` → `20260628-AM-01`; in `Effort`, find the `20200628-AM-01` row and make the same two edits (or delete both sets of rows and re-import once the fix deploys). **Owner live re-test:** re-import the same screenshot → expect the ⚠️ "reads 2020-06-28, which doesn't look right" warning + picker (if the model still returns 2020), or the plain no-date fallback (if the prompt fix holds); either way the save cannot land in 2020.
- ✅ **[correctness] Effort-screenshot save preview never shows the workout date — or the today-fallback warning — on the conversation surface (B5 regression)** — **FIXED (B5 conversation-surface PR, 2026-07-02).** The fix found a SECOND, worse defect while building: the screenshot-preview branch (`public/app.js`) always sent the auto-filled `#log-date` default (today) as an explicit `date` form field — and a manual date beats the screenshot date server-side (`resolveWorkoutDate`), so the screenshot's own date could NEVER win on this path and `date_source` read `'manual'`, suppressing even the today-fallback warning. **Shipped:** (1) the screenshot preview now sends `date` ONLY when the owner actually typed one (`logDateManuallyEntered`, RC2's flag; mirrors the RC5 blank-session_id pattern) — blank lets the server resolve screenshot-date → today-fallback and report an honest `date_source`; preview↔write parity holds because `pendingWrite.date` captures the server-RESOLVED date, which approve re-sends. (2) `emitCoachPreview` → `atlas:preview-ready` now carries `dateInfo { date, source }` from both preview renderers, and `buildReviewCard` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) renders it: header date falls back to the resolved date on effort-only cards (never dateless), `screenshot`/`manual` render a quiet confirmation line, and `today_fallback` renders the ⚠️ warning **plus an inline date input + "Use this date"** that sets `#log-date` via a real `input` event (latching `logDateManuallyEntered`) and re-dispatches the logger-form submit — re-running the SAME preview flow so payload, session id, and card re-resolve on the corrected date (read-only; the approve gate is unchanged). Plain set-log previews pass `source: null` → no notice, unchanged look. Tests: 3 introspection blocks in `test/unit.test.js` (date gating incl. manual-effort-path-unchanged; both renderers pass date+source; card renders notice + correction wiring). Shell v79→v80. **Owner live re-test:** re-import the July-28 screenshot → card shows "⚠️ No date found on the screenshot — saving as <today>" with a date picker; pick 2026-06-28 → "Use this date" → card re-previews with "Date: 2026-06-28" and a matching session id; save → Effort row lands on 06-28. _Original finding:_ Owner live test 07-02 (checklist B5): attached an Apple Watch screenshot dated July 28 (IMG_5125); the in-thread review card showed Duration / cals / HR + "Effort only — no sets logged this session" with **no date and no warning** — "it didn't seem to understand" the screenshot's date, and there is no way to see or correct the resolved date before approving. **Root cause:** the original B5 fix (`renderDateSourceNotice` + `date_source: manual|screenshot|today_fallback`) renders into the legacy `#preview-content` panel, but the conversation surface hides that panel (`previewPanel.hidden = true`, `public/app.js` ~5599) and makes the in-thread review card the single review surface — and `emitCoachPreview` (`public/app.js` ~3944) forwards only `rows/liftCodes/effortOnly/effort/substitutions`, not `data.date`/`data.date_source`, while `buildReviewCard` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) derives its date solely from `rows[0][0]`, which is empty on an effort-only save. So whether the server used the screenshot date or silently fell back to today, the card shows neither. Compounding — **owner confirmed 07-02: the screenshot header reads "July 28" with NO year** — ambiguous by design under the vision prompt's strict rule (`services/vision.js`: return a date only when visible AND unambiguous, as YYYY-MM-DD), so the silent today-fallback IS the live behavior, and the fix must include the B5 "ambiguous date asks" affordance (surface the fallback + a working correction path), not just date visibility. **Smallest safe fix (display-only; no write-path/trust-loop/schema/parser change):** thread `date` + `date_source` through `emitCoachPreview` → `atlas:preview-ready` → `buildReviewCard`, and render the resolved date plus the existing `renderDateSourceNotice` copy (⚠️ today-fallback warning / "Date from screenshot: X") on the review card. Same-PR design point: the conversation surface has no date-correction affordance for an effort-only save (the `#log-date` field lives on the hidden legacy form) — the warning copy must point at a correction path that actually works there. Touches `public/app.js` (event payload only) + `public/coach-conversation.js` (render) — high-risk-file discipline applies (tiny PR, focused diff, tests).
- 🔴 **CONFIRMED lost-sets (owner answer + retest IMG_5441/5442, 07-02 7:51): the five pasted exercises were NEVER saved.** The first paste got a fluent LLM readback ("I've got Bench Press 135 × 10 reps @ RIR 5 … what's your main goal for this session?") that *sounded* logged but buffered nothing; "Done" produced nothing; an expanded re-paste (one set per line) hit the generic coach fallback ("Noted — keep logging and say 'log it'"); "Log it" then failed with **"Couldn't find any sets in the conversation."** Five exercises acknowledged twice, zero rows buffered or saved — the trust path silently discarded the input and the LLM readback actively masked it. This is the known **Multi-workout composer bug (1)** re-confirmed for a third paste shape — see the 2026-07-02 re-test bullet added to that section below. **That lane is now the top bug priority, ahead of the B5 date fix.** _(Fixed same day — PR #794; see that bullet.)_
  - **Deferred — "Fix A" local-multiline fallback ordering on a none-resolved paste** `[housekeeping]` (Claude review PR #794, non-blocking): in the none-resolved case the result keeps `multiple_exercises_in_input`, so `err.multipleExercises` stays true and the pre-existing client-side local-multiline fallback (`public/app.js` ~3789) runs the crude local parser BEFORE the new specific-ask gate. The local parser's own `!multi.errors.length` clean-gate prevents mis-logging, so this is low-risk and pre-existing — but the ordering means a none-resolved paste the local parser happens to read cleanly logs via the LOCAL grammar instead of surfacing the server's specific ask. Revisit when that fallback path is next touched (consider running the specific-ask gate first, or deleting the local fallback once the server rescue has soaked).
- **Curls card-vs-advisory inconsistency `[polish]` (owner follow-up 07-02):** the ✓ confirmation card echoed `Curls` (parsed-name echo) while the advisory simultaneously said "I don't recognize 'Curls'" — two checks (set-parse vs catalog-enrichment) shown with contradictory confidence on one surface; if saved, the row writes `exercise: Curls` with `muscle_group: Unknown`. Catalog variants for BC01 are `Bicep Curl / Bicep Curls / Biceps Curl / Biceps Curls` — bare `Curls` is not among them ("Bicep curls" resolved cleanly in the same session). Two-part resolution: **(a) display — ✅ FIXED (review-card truthfulness PR, 2026-07-02):** when the unknown-lift advisory fires, the parsed name is remembered (`lastUnverifiedExercise`, `public/app.js`), threaded into the `atlas:set-logged` detail, and the confirmation card (`buildReadback`, `public/coach-conversation.js`) renders a "check name" chip next to the ✓ for that exercise (primary and additional lifts) — the card and the advisory now agree; the sets are still captured and the name stays editable before save. **Owner live-verified 2026-07-02 9:26 (IMG_5449): chip renders on the live ✓ Curls card alongside the advisory, shell v81 confirmed.** Same PR: the effort-only review intro no longer promises "the full session" (says plainly "no logged sets are in this save"), and an empty-string HR can no longer render as a bare "bpm" on the effort grid (empty ≡ null: Avg HR row drops, Peak HR shows "not in screenshot"). Shell v80→v81; introspection tests for all three. **(b) data — owner approved 2026-07-02**, but this cloud session has no Sheets credentials: owner runs `scripts/catalog-maintenance.js` (add variant `Curls` to canonical `Bicep Curl`, dry-run first, then `--confirm`) on a host with the Sheets env. Until the variant lands, bare `Curls` saves as `exercise: Curls` / `muscle_group: Unknown` — now clearly marked on the card.

## Bug remediation — 2026-06-29 live gym test (Bug_Reports tab, BUG-20260629)

Triaged from owner-logged bugs in the `Bug_Reports` Google Sheet tab (2026-06-26/29). Ordered by severity.

- ✅ **[data] All five campaign lifts verified present & correct in the production `Exercise_Catalog` (`1XQa…DuDcA0`)** (verified live 2026-06-29 via read-only CSV review). Columns are `Canonical_Name | Muscle_Group | Lift_Code | Original_Variants`. Confirmed rows: `Incline Bench Press | Chest | IBP01`, `Decline Bench Press | Chest | DBP01`, `Cable Tricep Pushdown | Arms | CTP01`, `Romanian Deadlift | Posterior Chain | RDL01` (variants incl. `RDL`, `RDLs` — matches the parser's `RDL` canonical), and `Bicep Curl | Biceps | BC01 | Bicep Curl, Bicep Curls, Biceps Curl, Biceps Curls` (added by owner at row 159). All canonical names match the parser output, so these lifts attribute correctly (no `Unknown`). Notes: (1) the earlier `113Ev…` edits were to a **stray** workbook — production is `1XQa…`; the catalog is a comprehensive ~239-row pre-built taxonomy, so Incline/Decline/Cable Tricep were already present there. (2) Enrichment is write-time — any sets logged as `Unknown` *before* a row existed won't self-correct; fix `muscle_group` by hand if history matters. (3) Earlier guidance in this file mis-stated the catalog as `Arms`/`Legs` and the wrong column order — corrected here and in `docs/SHEET_CONTRACT.md`. **Open follow-up:** `services/substitutionRecommender.js` references `'Incline Press'` (not `'Incline Bench Press'`) — a pre-existing substitution-suggestion naming inconsistency; correct in a future authorized parser/catalog-naming sweep (does not affect logging/attribution).
- ✅ **[tooling] Controlled `Exercise_Catalog` maintenance script** `[housekeeping]` — `scripts/catalog-maintenance.js` (owner-authorized 2026-06-29: "add or use a controlled catalog-maintenance command to append Exercise_Catalog rows"). Appends rows via the existing `sheets.js` client. **This is a new write-capable path to production Sheets** — deliberately gated: `Exercise_Catalog`-only (refuses other tabs; hard-blocks `Log_Cleaned`/`Effort`/`Bug_Reports`/…), dry-run by default, `--confirm` required to write, canonical+variant duplicate check, post-write read-back, never prints secrets. Pure planner unit-tested (13 cases) without creds. Production writes remain an explicit owner-initiated action (`--confirm` on a host that has the Sheets env). Does not touch runtime app code or the trust path.
  - **Deferred polish (review PR #693, non-blocking) `[housekeeping]`:** (1) dedup is name/variant-only — two rows with distinct names but the same `Lift_Code` could both write (catalog identity is by name, so low risk); (2) read-back verifies canonical-name presence only, not that `muscle_group`/`lift_code` landed in the right columns; (3) `getHeaderRow()` is redundant since `getExerciseCatalog()` already returns the header at index 0. Fold into a future authorized tooling touch — not worth a standalone PR.
- ✅ **[correctness] Bodyweight lifts no longer dead-end on "No recent working sets found for this lift code"** (PR #700) — `recommendNextSet` (`services/analytics.js`) filtered candidate working sets on a positive *weight*, which a bodyweight movement (hanging knee raises, push-ups) never has, so every set was dropped → false "no working sets" + no next card (BUG-20260629-153217 / 152824). Now gated on reps; a rep-based `isBodyweight` branch progresses by reps and never fabricates a load; bodyweight lifts emit a `next_target` so the card renders. Weighted lifts (incl. a stray trailing 0-weight set on a normally-weighted lift) stay on the load path via the `hasWeightedHistory` anchor. Read-only analytics path — no write/trust-loop/schema/proof-field change.
  - **Open follow-up (review PR #700, non-blocking) `[correctness]`:** a **first-ever in-workout bodyweight lift with NO history** still hits the original dead-end — when `rows.length === 0` the just-logged early-return anchor (`recommendNextSet` line ~613 → `recommendFromJustLoggedSet`) requires `isPositiveFinite(jl.weight)`, so a brand-new bodyweight set returns "No recent working sets found" + `next_target: null`. PR #700 fixed the read-back-with-history path (the reported live bug); this no-history just-logged case needs `recommendFromJustLoggedSet` to grow a rep-based branch. Distinct code path → deferred to a future authorized analytics PR.
- ✅ **[correctness] A single bare bodyweight rep logs as one set — "missed rows" in a multi-exercise paste** (PR pending) — `BUG-20260629-152824` ("I put a bunch of workouts in at once and it missed rows"). Verification gate: the *originally-reported* missed-rows symptom on build `95c5b78` predates **both** #699 (multiline-merge fix) and #700, and maps to the #699 "a second exercise's set merges into the prior lift" bug = **already fixed**. While verifying, found a *separate* still-open trigger: a lone bare rep on an unambiguously-bodyweight lift (`push ups 20`) fell through to a "missing sets" dead-end (`parseBareBodyweightReps` required ≥2 tokens), and because a multi-exercise block is all-clean-or-drop, that one unresolved segment discarded every sibling row. Fixed in `services/workoutTextParser.js` by accepting a single bare rep for the Push-Up path (only caller; Push-Up never carries load, so a lone integer is unambiguously reps). Knee-raises/Dips bare numbers stay ambiguous-by-design (golden-tested) and still ask. Pure parser fix — no write/trust-loop/schema/proof change.
- **[correctness] Multi-exercise freestyle is all-clean-or-drop — one ambiguous lift discards the clean siblings** `[deferred — trust-design]` — when a multi-exercise paste contains a genuinely ambiguous lift (e.g. `knee raises 20 15 15` — weight vs reps, deliberately clarified), the current `parseLogSets` returns `needs_clarification` for the **whole** block and logs none of the cleanly-parsed exercises (`services/workoutTextParser.js` ~line 560, "only return the combined result if EVERY chunk cleanly resolves"). This is deliberately conservative (never mis-log), but it reads to the lifter as "missed rows". A partial-log path (log the clean lifts into the preview, surface only the ambiguous one to re-enter) would resolve the remaining "missed rows" class — but it touches the preview→approve trust surface, so it is a **design decision for the owner**, not a silent change. **OWNER DECIDED 2026-07-02: partial-log.** Shipped for **newline-separated** pastes in the partial-log PR (see the ✅ 2026-07-02 re-test bullet in "Multi-workout composer" below): clean lines buffer, each ambiguous line surfaces its own specific ask. **Remaining scope:** inline same-line stacking still refuses-to-merge whole (G1 guardrail — partial parsing is unsafe without a reliable inline boundary; extend partial-log there only with the owner-gated KB-backed splitter lane), and a single-line single-exercise ambiguous input (e.g. `knee raises 20 15 15`) still asks whole, as designed.

## Live-retest harness (manual, owner-driven) — owner-approved roadmap

Roadmap (owner, 2026-06-30): PR #715 scaffolding → #716 browser bootstrap → #717 scenario navigation (never Save) → #718 assertion engine (read-only) → #719 scenario-library expansion → #720 owner-experience reporting. **Future / NOT approved** (separate design + trust review required): production write automation, automatic Save, Google Sheets verification, automatic bug closure, merge gates based on live retests. One PR per slice; stop and open a PR at each step.

- ✅ **Slice 1 (PR #715) — manual workflow + scenario catalogue** `[housekeeping]` (shipped) — `.github/workflows/live-retest.yml` (`workflow_dispatch` only) + `scripts/live-retest.js`. Lines up a known *fixed-pending-live-retest* bug (`bug-20260629-153258`, `-153312`, `-003505`, `-002945`) and prints bug id / purpose / input / expected / forbidden (sourced from `docs/BUG_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md`). Validates inputs, fails on unknown scenario, **defaults to dry-run / no-write**. Not a merge gate, not in branch protection, not a replacement for owner judgment.
- ✅ **Slice 2 (PR #716) — read-only browser bootstrap** `[housekeeping]` (shipped) — `--dry-run-only false` now launches Playwright Chromium, opens the deployed Atlas `/app/`, verifies the shell loaded (`body[data-surface]`), locates the composer (`#workout-text`), confirms the write button (`#approve-btn`) is **present and disabled** (observed, never clicked), captures a full-page screenshot to `live-retest-artifacts/` (gitignored; uploaded as a CI artifact), and exits. **READ-ONLY**: no composer fill, no preview/approve click, no write endpoint, no Sheets write. The workflow installs Chromium only when a non-dry-run bootstrap is requested. Dry-run remains the default and is unchanged. No runtime app change. Locally verified against `tests/e2e/static-server.js`.
- ✅ **Slice 3 (PR #717) — scenario navigation (composer + preview, never Save)** `[correctness]` (shipped) — the read-only browser run now drives the scenario through the UI: each scenario carries a `navigation` descriptor; composer-type scenarios populate `#workout-text` with the scenario text and submit the **preview** flow only (a `test_mode` dry-run — no write), capturing `02-composer` / `03-preview` screenshots and the post-preview thread state. The restore-banner scenario (`-003505`) is tagged `manual` (a UI-state action, not composer-driven) and cleanly skips composer navigation. **Hard read-only guard: only `#workout-text` and `#preview-btn` are ever touched; `#approve-btn` is observed-and-confirmed-disabled, never enabled or clicked** — re-asserted after the preview. No write endpoint, no Sheets write, no runtime app change. Verified locally against `tests/e2e/static-server.js` (composer populated, preview navigated, approve never clicked, exit 0).
- ✅ **Slice 4 (PR #718) — read-only assertion engine** `[correctness]` (shipped) — each scenario gains an `assertion: { forbidden:[...], expected:[...] }`; after navigation, `assertScenario` compares the captured post-preview thread text against the patterns and emits a verdict — **FAIL** (a forbidden/bug pattern reappeared → exit 2), **PASS** (no forbidden + expected present, or a real response when no expected patterns), **INCONCLUSIVE** (no forbidden but expected missing — e.g. an unauthenticated run), **MANUAL** (restore-banner, no automatable assertion). Writes a `<scenario>-result.json` artifact (verdict + per-pattern matched + thread excerpt + screenshot list) alongside the screenshots. Pure string/regex comparison over already-captured text — no extra navigation, **no writes**. Verified locally: `-153258` → PASS (forbidden absent; thread shows the correct fixed reply), `-002945` → INCONCLUSIVE (needs an authed parse), `-003505` → MANUAL.
- ✅ **Slice 5 (PR #719) — scenario-library expansion + reporting** `[correctness]` (shipped) — the catalogue now covers **all five** 🟡 yellow `BUG_TRIAGE_LEDGER` rows: added `bug-20260629-204817` (recovery-bump recurrence — multi-step Coach's-Pick flow, tagged `manual` for now). New **`all`** mode runs every scenario in sequence and prints a verdict summary table + writes `summary.json` (PASS/FAIL/INCONCLUSIVE/MANUAL tally); `all` and `-204817` added to the workflow `scenario` choices. Also folded in the two #718 review tighten-ups: (a) the result manifest now lists only screenshots that exist on disk (a MANUAL scenario lists just `01-loaded`); (b) the coach-fallback rows gained a positive `expected` pattern (`re-?type` / `add it to the preview`) so an unauthenticated error toast reads **INCONCLUSIVE**, not a false PASS. Still strictly read-only — never Save, no writes. Verified locally (`all` → PASS=2/MANUAL=2/INCONCLUSIVE=1) + `test/live-retest-assert.test.js` (8 unit tests over the verdict matrix).
- ✅ **Slice 6 (PR #720) — owner-experience reporting** `[housekeeping]` (shipped) — `writeSummary` now also emits `summary.md`, a friendly markdown verdict report (`buildMarkdownReport`): a table of verdict-icon · BUG-id (linked to a commit search when run in Actions) · purpose, a totals line, a verdict legend, and the read-only / not-a-merge-gate disclaimer. The workflow renders it into the **GitHub Actions run summary** (`$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY`, always() so a FAIL still shows), with a dry-run note when no report exists; the artifact upload (`live-retest-artifacts-*`) now carries the screenshots + result JSON + summary. 3 new unit tests over `buildMarkdownReport`/`writeSummary` (closing the #719 nit). Pure/read-only — no Save, no writes, no runtime change. **Live-retest roadmap (#715–#720) complete.**
  - **Authenticated preview rendering** (owner-gated): injecting an owner API key so the dry-run preview executes server-side for the composer scenarios — separate decision (credential handling); still no write either way. The coach-fallback rows submit via the unified composer/`#preview-btn` which routes by intent.
  - **Future / NOT approved (owner-gated, separate design):** production write automation, automatic Save, Sheets verification, automatic bug closure, merge gates on live retests. Do not build without an explicit owner decision and a hard no-production-write contract.
