## Multi-workout composer (owner 2026-06-25: "log two or more workouts at once")

`[correctness]` Owner wants to paste several workouts at once, have Atlas recognize each, and confirm with one card. **Live sample captured (IMG_4895/4896/4897, 2026-06-25):** the real composer paste is `Deadlift\n135lbs 10 · warm-up\n…\n245lbs 6/2\n…\nBench Press\n…` — i.e. bare exercise-name headers, warm-up set lines `{weight}lbs {reps} · warm-up`, and working set lines in Atlas slash notation `{weight}lbs {reps}/{rir}` (NOT the `135 lb × 10` Strong/Hevy format originally assumed). Live bugs in that test: (1) the multi-exercise paste was not recognized as sets — it hit the coach fallback "Noted — keep logging" (`coach-conversation.js:1588`) and closeout then said "no sets logged"; (2) warm-ups were logged as working sets with a **fabricated RIR 4** and counted with no distinction.

- ✅ **Live re-test 2026-07-02 (owner, IMG_5441/5442) — bug (1) re-confirmed for a THIRD paste shape; sets LOST. FIXED (partial-log PR, 2026-07-02).** `[trust-critical]` **Fix:** root cause was `normalizeParserText` collapsing newlines BEFORE parsing, so a one-exercise-per-line paste reached `parseLogSets` as a single blob and dead-ended on the generic multi-exercise ask (whose own advice — "put each exercise on its own line" — was therefore unfulfillable). `parseWorkoutText` (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) now runs a multi-line rescue when the collapsed whole-text parse dead-ends (`multiple_exercises_in_input` / `unattributable_trailing_sets`): `parseMultilineLogSets` re-parses per line through the proven single-entry parser (slash notation, ×N/xN, bodyweight, multi-set lines, bare-name headers with context carry) — resolved lines return as `log_sets_multi`, unresolved lines return in `unresolved` with each line's own SPECIFIC ask (**partial-log, owner decision 2026-07-02** — supersedes all-clean-or-drop for newline-separated pastes; inline same-line stacking keeps the G1 refuse-to-merge guardrail pending the owner-gated KB-backed splitter lane). Client (`public/app.js`): partial responses buffer the clean rows and surface "one line needs a check … Which row — seated, bent-over…?"; a none-resolved slash-notation paste surfaces its first specific ask instead of routing to the coach — set notation can no longer sink into a fluent LLM readback over an empty buffer. The rescue runs ONLY on inputs that dead-ended before; every currently-succeeding path is byte-identical. Tests: `test/multilinePartialLog.test.js` (11 golden, incl. the exact owner repro + unicode ×), `test/parserStackedBoundary.test.js` updated to the partial-log contract (hard never-absorb invariant unchanged), 3 client-wiring tests in `test/unit.test.js`. Shell v78→v79. **Owner live re-test:** paste the same five-exercise block → 4 exercises buffer with cards + "Which row…?" ask; reply "seated rows 150 10/2 x3" → attaches; save → 14 rows. _Original finding:_ Paste shape: one exercise per LINE in Atlas slash notation with the `×N` multiplier — `Bench 135 10/5 185 8/2` / `Rows 150 10/2 ×3` / `Dips 10/2 ×3` / `Bicep curls 30 12/2 ×3` / `KR 15/2 ×3`. This is NOT the display-block format PR #591 wired (bare-name headers + `135lbs 10` lines) and NOT the shipped single-line inline (way-2) case. Observed: first paste → fluent LLM readback (all five lifts correctly named, incl. "Knee Raises" from `KR`) that sounded logged but buffered NOTHING; "Done" → nothing; expanded re-paste (one set per line) → generic coach fallback ("Noted — keep logging and say 'log it'"); "Log it" → "Couldn't find any sets in the conversation." Zero rows buffered or saved across the whole exchange. Likely mechanics to verify with /investigate before fixing: bare/ambiguous name-lines (`Rows` is ambiguous-by-design per the G1 extension; `KR` may not be a parser alias) tripping the all-clean-or-drop rule (see the deferred all-clean-or-drop item in "Bug remediation — 2026-06-29"), with the whole block then falling through to the chat LLM instead of a clarification. **Fix requirements (Atlas-principle violations — deterministically-loggable input must not route to the coach; the trust path must not silently discard user intent):** (a) this paste shape parses (or splits per-line into the proven per-exercise parser); (b) an ambiguous chunk surfaces its SPECIFIC clarification — never the generic keep-logging fallback, and never a fluent LLM readback over an empty buffer; (c) the "log it"/"done" empty-buffer copy must not claim "no sets in the conversation" when the user pasted sets that failed routing. **Blocking design question for the owner (already filed as the all-clean-or-drop item): when 4 of 5 lines are clean, may Atlas preview the clean 4 and ask only about the 5th (partial-log), or must it keep all-or-nothing?** The fix's shape depends on this answer.

- ✅ **Wiring slice 1 — warm-up tag convention + progression exclusion** (PR #590) — new `services/warmupTag.js` (single source of truth): `WARMUP_NOTE_TOKEN`, `isWarmupNote(notes)`, `tagWarmupNote(notes)`. A warm-up is marked by an explicit token in the `notes` column (owner-approved notes/status mechanism — no schema change) instead of a fabricated RIR. Wired `isWarmupNote` into all **five** progression/trajectory consumers (`exerciseBenchmark`, `expectedPerformance`, `coachMemory`, `trendDetector`, and — most live-path-relevant — `analytics.recommendNextSet`'s `progressionBand` + e1RM `sessionBests`, the primary "Next" weight-bump engine wired into `index.js`; both added per PR #590 review) so a note-tagged warm-up is excluded from the working-set / weight-bump / trend decision **even when it is heavy** — closing the heuristic hole (a `225×8` warm-up at >60% of a 245 top set used to leak in). Volume is untouched (warm-ups still count). Additive/inert until a producer tags rows (slices 2/3). 7 tests in `test/warmupTag.test.js`. Deterministic, server-side, no trust-loop/write-path-mechanics/proof-field change.
    - **Follow-up for the producer (slices 2/3), review note PR #590** `[correctness]`: `WARMUP_NOTE_RE` (`/\bwarm[\s-]?up\b|\bwu\b|\(w\)/i`) has a small false-positive surface — a genuine *working*-set note containing "warm up" (e.g. "good warm up today") would be excluded from progression. Inert today (no producer). When slices 2/3 write the marker, have the producer emit a **controlled/structured token** (e.g. a reserved leading marker) that won't collide with owner free-text, and consider tightening detection to that token for Atlas-written rows while keeping lenient parsing for inbound composer text. Low risk; not a blocker for the inert read-side PR.
    - ✅ **RESOLVED (PR #593)** — review note PR #590 #2: `analytics.recommendNextSet`'s immediate bump anchor (`lastSet`/`priorSet`) now prefers **working sets** (`workingRows = rows.filter(!isWarmupNote)`, falling back to all rows only if every row is a warm-up), so a warm-up logged *last* can't drive "increase to X". Behavioral control-paired test added.
    - ✅ **RESOLVED (PR #593)** — review note PR #590 #3: `sessionBestByLift` + `historicalBestByLift` now skip note-tagged warm-ups, so a heavy tagged feeler no longer registers a false personal record; the PR baseline is working sets only.
    - ✅ **RESOLVED (PR #593)** — review note PR #593: the last two LIVE PR readers also skip tagged warm-ups — `detectRecentPrs` (GET `/api/prs/recent`) filters them at the row level, and `buildWeeklyReport`'s weekly-PR block skips them in both the this-week and prior-week best maps. With these, **all** progression/trajectory/PR surfaces (benchmark, expected-perf, coachMemory, trendDetector, recommendNextSet band+anchor, sessionBest/historicalBest, detectRecentPrs, weekly report) exclude note-tagged warm-ups; volume still counts them.
    - ✅ **RESOLVED (warm-up handling rule PR, 2026-06-25)** — closed the last gap: `detectStalls` (and therefore `suggestDeloads`, which reads `stall.last_best_weight` for the deload weight) excluded warm-ups from its per-session best, so a tagged heavy feeler could mask a real working stall or set the deload weight. Now `detectStalls` filters note-tagged warm-ups (a stall is a progression/working-set decision). With this, **every** progression/working-set decision excludes warm-ups while volume (`computeMuscleGroupVolume`) still counts them. New end-to-end contract test `test/warmupHandlingRule.test.js` locks all five sub-rules (saved as normal rows; marked via notes; counted in volume; excluded from progression incl. stalls/deload; no fabricated RIR). Classification/accounting only — the composer/write-path tagging stays the deferred P0 slice (`tagWarmupNote` is ready but unwired).
  - ✅ **Inline multi-exercise (way-2) — "log it however"** (this PR) — owner 2026-06-25: "the user should be able to log whatever way in the composer and Atlas should know what to do with it." The proven parser used to BLOCK several exercises in one message (`multiple_exercises_in_input` → clarification). Now `parseLogSets` SPLITS on the recognized exercise names (`splitMultiExerciseSegments`, using `findExerciseMentions`) and parses each chunk independently, returning a new `log_sets_multi` intent (`{ exercises: [...] }`). So `Deadlift 135 10/4 185 10/2 245 6/1 5/1 4/2 bench 135 10/5 …` (inline, one line) logs both lifts, each with its OWN weight (no row leak — verified). **Never mis-logs:** only returns the combined result if EVERY chunk cleanly resolves to real sets; any ambiguous/sets-less chunk (or a token-count mismatch making indices unreliable) falls back to the clarification ask. Splits on KNOWN exercise names only (catalog/aliases) — an unrecognized inline lift can't be a reliable boundary, so that asks. Client (`rowsFromBackendParsedWorkout`) flattens `log_sets_multi` into rows → the same preview→approve→write loop. Tests: `test/inlineMultiExercise.test.js` + updated the 5 prior "block multi-exercise" tests to the split-and-log contract + api-smoke dry-run. **NEEDS OWNER LIVE RE-TEST.** Remaining: an unknown-exercise inline boundary still asks; per-exercise coaching narration not separately wired.
  - ✅ **RDL / RDLs / Romanian Deadlift normalization** `[correctness]` (this PR) — **Verdict: BROKEN → fixed.** Owner live evidence (B2 Slice 2): "RDLs" carded + warned + **saved with muscle group Unknown**. Root cause: the parser's own `EXERCISE_ALIASES` (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) had only singular RDL forms (`romanian deadlift`, `romanian dl`, `rdl`) — no plurals — so `RDLs` / `Romanian Deadlifts` / `rdls` fell through to `exercise: "Rdls"` with `warnings: ['unknown_exercise']` + `needs_catalog_review: true`, and muscle-group enrichment had nothing to match → `Unknown`. (The Exercise-KB JSON `data/exercise_aliases.v1.json` already had `rdls`/`romanian deadlifts`, but the parser's hardcoded list is a *separate* alias system and was missing them.) Fix: added `romanian deadlifts`, `romanian dls`, `rdls` to the parser's RDL alias array (longest-first so plurals win over singulars). Now every RDL form canonicalizes to `RDL` with no warning, identical to the singular. **Pure parser alias addition — NO KB→parser coupling** (per the owner's hold on that boundary). Regression-verified: plain `Deadlift`/`dl` still resolve to `Deadlift`, never RDL. Tests: 5 golden cases in `test/parser-golden.test.js` (singular clean, plural normalizes, full-name plural, lowercase plural, no Deadlift cross-contamination). No write-path / schema / proof-field / trust-loop change.
    - **Discovered follow-up — plural `deadlifts` is unrecognized** `[correctness]`: same class of gap — the `Deadlift` alias array (`['deadlift', 'dead', 'dl']`) has no plural, so `deadlifts 315 5` parses as `exercise: "Deadlifts"` (`unknown_exercise`). Out of scope for the RDL-only PR; file for a future plural-normalization sweep (consider auditing all alias arrays for missing plurals, or a general trailing-`s` fallback that defers to explicit aliases first). Low risk; parser-only.
  - ◐ **G1 — never silently merge a stacked second exercise (refuse-to-merge guardrail)** `[trust-critical / data-integrity]` (owner-gated parser) — closes the gap the item above left open: when the SECOND stacked exercise's name is NOT a recognized alias (e.g. `Weighted Dips 50 11/1 x3 Dumbbell Side Bend 70 15/1 x3`), `splitMultiExerciseSegments` can't see it as a boundary, so the backend parser previously **merged** the second lift's sets into the first ("Dips") — the trailing `70×15` became a fabricated Dips PR (live finding F3/F5, 2026-06-25; the trust loop held — nothing was approved/written — so this was preview/coach integrity, and the fix prevents a future approved session writing a phantom PR). **Fix shipped:** `services/workoutTextParser.js` detects an unattributable trailing set-group — a set-group separated from the first by a word run that is *entirely* exercise-name-like (no set-notation/continuation-filler/number-word/contextual-alias word) — and **surfaces it as `needs_clarification` (`unattributable_trailing_sets`)** instead of absorbing it. NEVER silently merges. **Pure structure, NO Exercise-KB coupling** (owner decision 2026-06-25: hold the KB→parser boundary) and **zero existing parser tests changed** (a structural *split* approach flipped 10 tests on natural-language notation — `for`/`reps`/`lb`/`all around RIR`/`x` — proving pure structure can't tell notation from a name without a dictionary, so split was rejected in favor of the guardrail). Tests: `test/parserStackedBoundary.test.js` (repro no-merge + annotation/continuation/natural-language false-positive guards + alias-multi unchanged + backend↔normalizer consistency). Live test: **LT-003**. **This is the guardrail, not the feature** → next item.
    - ✅ **G1 extension — ambiguous-alias second exercise + `seated rows` plural (BUG-20260629 multiline freestyle, this PR).** Live repro: `bench 135 8/2 / rows 95 10/2 / dips 10/2 / knee raises 15/2` logged the `95×10` **rows** set UNDER Bench Press (card showed `135×8 · 95×10`); the coach only *narrated* a fix, never corrected the card/state. Root: two gaps. (1) The G1 refuse-to-merge guard excluded **`AMBIGUOUS_ALIASES` keys** (`row`/`rows`/`press`) from its name-word test — so a bare ambiguous `rows` standing before its own set-group was treated as not-a-name and its set absorbed into the prior lift. Fix: build `CONTEXTUAL_ALIAS_WORDS` from `CONTEXTUAL_ALIASES` **only** (drop `Object.keys(AMBIGUOUS_ALIASES)`) — `row`/`rows` now count as name-like → G1 refuses-to-merge → `needs_clarification` (`press` stays excluded via the `incline press` contextual phrase). (2) `seated rows` (plural) wasn't a recognized alias → it fell to unknown `Seated Rows` and got bundled into bench; added the plurals `seated rows`/`cable rows`/`machine rows` to the `Seated Row` alias so a confidently-named row splits into its own entry. Now `rows` (ambiguous) → asks clarification / fails loudly, `seated rows` → its own `Seated Row [95×10]` card, and a bench card never carries a row set. Tests: 3 in `test/parserStackedBoundary.test.js` (the exact multiline repro + `seated rows` split + bare-`rows` single-entry guardrail) + 4 golden in `test/parser-golden.test.js` (plural row aliases resolve; bare `rows` stays ambiguous). Pure parser/structure — no Exercise-KB coupling, no write-path/trust-loop/schema/proof-field change. Does **not** build the stacked-split feature (still owner-gated, next item).
  - **Make stacked single-bubble input actually PARSE into separate exercises (the feature behind the G1 guardrail)** `[correctness]` `owner-decision: UX` — **OWNER DECISION 2026-06-28: SUPPORT stacking.** Atlas should eventually support one-bubble multi-exercise stacking when the second lift can be **confidently recognized by the Exercise KB** — matches the "log it however" direction. **Build it as its OWN separate PR/lane (a KB-backed stacked-bubble splitter), NOT inside any other PR; do not couple `services/workoutTextParser.js` ↔ the Exercise KB until that lane.** Until then the G1 refuse-to-merge guardrail stays (a stacked entry fails loudly rather than mis-merging). The G1 guardrail makes a stacked entry *fail loudly* (surfaced as unresolved), it does NOT make it *work* (two clean cards). Auto-splitting one bubble when the second name is out-of-catalog needs a **dictionary** boundary: the Exercise KB (`services/exerciseResolver.js`) resolves "dumbbell side bend" → Oblique Side Bend cleanly and returns null for notation like "all around rir"/"for", where pure structure can't. Smallest path when the lane opens: a KB-backed second-exercise detector (split when confidently recognized; else keep the refuse-to-merge guardrail).
  - ✅ **Wiring slice 2/3 — composer multi-exercise recognition** (PR #591) — wired the normalizer into `rowsFromWorkoutInput` (`public/app.js`): a display-block paste (`Deadlift\n135lbs 10 · warm-up\n…\n245lbs 6/2\n…\nBench Press\n…`) is detected via `displayBlockNormalizer.normalizeDisplayBlocks`, and rows are built **directly** from the parsed blocks (`rowsFromDisplayBlocks`) — exercise name passed through (server enriches canonical/lift_code/muscle on write), working RIR kept, **warm-ups tagged** in `notes` (`WARMUP_LOG_NOTE`, cross-checked against `services/warmupTag.isWarmupNote`). Rows flow through the **unchanged** preview→approve→write loop (the "combined confirm card" = the existing review card, which already lists all exercises + one Save). **kg gate**: a kg paste is refused with a convert-to-lb message, never silently logged as lb. Conservative: single-line / slash / ambiguous input still routes to the proven parser. Fixes the live repro (IMG_4904/4905, web): the multi-block paste no longer drops to the coach ("Noted — keep logging") with "no sets logged." Added `displayBlockNormalizer.js` to `index.html` + `sw.js` precache; shell v53→v54. Tests in `test/multiWorkoutComposerWiring.test.js`. **NEEDS OWNER LIVE RE-TEST** (touches the trust-loop input).
    - **Remaining (smaller, deferred):** (a) the **single-line coach-hint** form `DL 135x10wu 185x10wu 245 6/2 x3` still goes through the proven slash parser, which drops `wu` tokens (warm-ups not logged from that one-liner) — only the multi-LINE display block is wired here; wire the single-line `wu` path next if the owner uses it. (b) per-set coaching narration on a multi-exercise paste is not separately wired (the review card summarizes). (c) the producer-side follow-ups above (lastSet/priorSet anchor, PR/personal-best path) still apply once tagged rows are written by this path. **Owner decisions (2026-06-25) this was built to:**
    - **(a) Source format — DECIDED: fixture-driven, do not overfit.** Prioritize the real formats seen in Atlas live use: Atlas/chat-style slash notation; pasted multi-exercise blocks from the app/composer; Strong/Hevy-style export text *if a real sample is supplied later*. Build the normalizer extension to be **fixture-driven and easy to extend** (add a sample → add a case), not hard-coded to one app. Do not add a new app format without a real sample. (Review note PR #587: a trailing set-count multiplier on a display line — e.g. `135 × 10 x3` — currently keeps only the first `weight×reps` and ignores the `x3`; confirm whether the owner's real paste uses this and add a fixture if so.)
    - **(b) Warm-up logging — DECIDED & REFINED (owner 2026-06-25): log warm-ups, COUNT them in volume, exclude them only from the weight-bump/progression decision.** Owner's words: *"warm-ups should be included in volume just not included in when we decide to bump the weight up — those would be the working sets … in the log it should count the volume."* So: warm-up sets the user performed are real training history and **must be saved as rows** (use `canonicalText` / the full `sets[]`; the engine flags warm-ups via `set.warmup`, never drops them) and **DO count toward total session volume** (the review-card total already sums all sets — that is correct, leave it). They must **not** drive progression / weight-bump / "target hit" / RIR-trend — only **working sets** do. **Schema:** `Log_Cleaned` has no warm-up column and adding one is an owner-gated migration — so mark warm-ups via the existing **`notes`** mechanism (a `warm-up` marker) and **document the limitation**. **Builder recommendation (accepted direction):** do NOT stamp warm-ups with a fabricated RIR (e.g. the live "RIR 4") — Atlas never invents numbers the lifter didn't give; tag warm-ups explicitly via the note, keep them in volume, and have the progression engine skip note-tagged warm-ups. Note Atlas's existing progression heuristic (`exerciseBenchmark`/`expectedPerformance`: working = weight ≥60% top **and** RIR <4) has a hole — a heavy-ish warm-up (e.g. `225×8` warm-up at >60% of a 245 top set) would leak into progression — which is exactly why an explicit warm-up tag is needed, not just the weight/RIR heuristic. (`canonicalTextWorkingOnly` is the working-set *subset* for the progression decision only — NOT a log filter; the full set is still logged.)
    - **(c) kg handling (review note, PR #587):** the engine captures per-set `unit` (`lb`/`kg`) but `canonicalText`/`canonicalTextWorkingOnly` emit only `weight reps` — so a `60 kg × 8` paste becomes `"Name 60 8"`, which the lb-default `parseWorkoutText` would log as **60 lb** (a silent kg→lb corruption). Latent only while unwired; the wiring slice MUST convert kg→lb or refuse/warn on kg input **before** it reaches the write path (use the `unit` field already on each set).

## P0 coach-trust findings — owner live session 2026-06-25 (investigation, not yet fixed)

Six findings from the owner's 2026-06-25 live session. Full root-cause analysis with file:line evidence, root-cause groups, severity re-rank, and proposed fix order: [`docs/INVESTIGATION_2026-06-25.md`](./docs/INVESTIGATION_2026-06-25.md). **Investigation only — nothing fixed here.** Verified against code; the actual logged Sheets rows were NOT reachable in-session (see the doc's "Open questions for Dale").

- 🔴 **G1 (F3+F5) — parser exercise-boundary is alias-gated → merged sets + fabricated PR** `[trust-critical / data-integrity]` (owner-gated parser): a stacked second exercise whose name is **not in `EXERCISE_ALIASES`** (e.g. "Dumbbell Side Bend") is not recognized as a boundary, so `hasMultipleExerciseMentions` is false and the backend parser merges its sets into the first lift ("Dips (Weighted)") — the trailing `70×15` became a fake Dips PR. **F3** (split failed → one card) and **F5** (false PR off merged data) are the **same root**. Note the two intake paths diverge: the **client display-block normalizer** (`public/displayBlockNormalizer.js`) splits structurally (would split a *multi-line* paste), while the **backend parser** (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) is alias-gated (merges an *inline* one). Fix: structural boundary (name-then-sets) or refuse-to-merge fallback; never mis-log. Smallest stopgap = add the missing alias (mechanical) but that does NOT close the general defect. **Highest priority** — corrupts logged history AND fabricates coaching. Opus; needs a live-test card.
- **G3 (F2) — wrong "Next" + premature "say done"** `[trust-critical]`: "Next" pointed to Face Pull when the queued lift was Weighted Dips, and "say done to save" appeared while Single-Leg Curl was still queued. Two "next" notions exist (literal plan order `getNextExerciseInPlan` vs engine `next_move_advisory`, `coach-conversation.js:925/773`); the "done" invite/recap reads canonical-session `remaining`. **Primary mechanism is independent of the parse cluster** (reads plan/cursor/advisory state), but a G1 merge can leave a slot unadvanced. **Needs the live plan/cursor state to pin the cause before fixing** (Open question 3). Likely fix: disambiguate recommended-next vs next-in-queue; gate "say done" on `remaining.length===0`. Opus; needs a live-test card. **Live repro (2026-06-26, FA/LT-005 run):** owner logged a 2-lift plan (Back Squat + Overhead Press); after both, Atlas rendered **"Plan complete. Say 'done' or take a screenshot to save."** while the owner had **more workouts to log** ("I'm not done, these are the first two"). Root is `planIsComplete` firing when all *planned* lifts are done — but the lifter intended to log **beyond the plan**, so "complete" is premature from their view. This is the same "what actually ends a session" surface as **FB** (screenshot-as-done): the completion model conflates *plan exhausted* with *lifter done*. **Tackle G3 + FB together** as the completion-flow slice — needs the owner's FB decision (preview vs one-tap save) and likely a way to keep logging past a finished plan without the closeout nagging. Completion-flow = trust-critical; own scoped PR + model hold point, not a drive-by. **Update (2026-06-26):** the premature "say done" wording was fixed in #612 (G3 copy — "planned work done, keep logging or save", never "session over"). **The off-plan "Next" is fixed in `fix/off-plan-next-up-handoff` (PR A):** the live "next up: Hammer Curls" (a lift NOT in today's plan) came from the `getNextExerciseInPlan` → `/api/plan/today` fallback surfacing a stored-program lift; the handoff now rejects any fallback next-up that isn't in the engaged plan (`detail.plannedOrder`), so freestyle logging stays clean and, when the engaged plan is already complete, the closeout fires instead of an off-plan handoff.
  - ✅ **Follow-up (PR A discovery): repeated "Planned work done" on every post-plan log** `[polish]` — Added `closeoutAnnounced` boolean guard (analogous to `lastAnnouncedNextUp`) in `public/coach-conversation.js`. The closeout div now renders only once per session; subsequent off-plan sets after plan exhaustion get no repeated announcement. Guard resets to `false` on `atlas:session-reset` (alongside `lastAnnouncedNextUp`) so a fresh session can show the closeout again. Coach-render layer only; no write/schema/trust-loop change. Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (source-introspection assertions for guard declaration, conditional wrapper, `true`-set, and reset-listener wiring).
  - **Follow-up (PR A review #613, non-blocking): behavioral test for the off-plan handoff rejection** `[polish]`: the off-plan rejection is currently covered by source-introspection (`unit.test.js`), matching the existing "never resurrects a completed lift" test style; `postLogIdentity.test.js` only asserts `plannedOrder` is emitted. A behavioral test that drives `handleSetLogged` with an off-plan `nextEx` and asserts it's nulled would harden against regression — but `handleSetLogged` is DOM-bound with no existing harness (would need a DOM mock or extracting the inline guard to a helper). Add when the coach-render layer next gets a behavioral harness. Test-only; no behavior change.
- **Open questions for Dale (block precise confirmation):** (1) exact input text for the Dips+Side Bend bubble (multi-line vs inline); (2) the actual logged Sheets rows for it (merged into Dips? own row? both?); (3) the plan + cursor/session state when "Next: Face Pull" appeared and at the "say done" moment; (4) whether the coach LLM was live or degraded during the session. See `docs/INVESTIGATION_2026-06-25.md`.

## Coach render-order & completion-flow — owner live session 2026-06-25 (2nd run, investigation)

Trust fixes verified PASS live this run (G1 no-merge, G2 both lifts coached, G4 no fabricated units). Two remaining issues are render-order and completion-flow. Full analysis with file:line evidence, root-cause groups, and proposed fix order: [`docs/INVESTIGATION_2026-06-25b.md`](./docs/INVESTIGATION_2026-06-25b.md). **Investigation only — nothing fixed here.**

- ◐ **FB — screenshot upload should count as "done"** `[trust-critical / completion-flow]` — **REOPENED 2026-06-28: the owner live test FAILED.** The screenshot upload DID open a preview (the #612 trigger works), but two trust-critical defects surfaced on the closeout-screenshot save path (owner evidence IMG_5113–5118):
  - **RC1 — preview ≠ write (silent row drops).** The preview showed 7 exercises / 30 sets / 33,885 lb, but the sheet saved only **22** Log_Cleaned rows for `20260628-PM-01`, with **Bench Press dropped entirely** and Seated Row written ×3 (preview showed ×6). Root: the `/api/log-workout` row-level composite-key dedup (`session‖exercise‖set_number`) ran ONLY on the live write; the `test_mode` dry-run returned the RAW `formattedLogRows`, so the preview promised rows the live write then silently skipped (`index.js`). **✅ FIXED (this PR — preview-write parity):** extracted `partitionLogRowsByExisting(formattedRows, existingLogKeys)` and applied it in BOTH the dry-run preview and the live write (shared code = guaranteed parity), so `log_rows_preview` now shows exactly the rows that will be appended and surfaces `skipped_duplicates`. Tests: `test/api-smoke.test.js` "FB parity: …" (preview applies dedup; preview count == live write count). Server-only; no trust-loop/proof-field/schema/parser change.
  - ◐ **RC2 — screenshot date ignored** `[trust-critical]` — **✅ FIXED (this PR — owner decision: auto-use the screenshot date); awaiting owner LIVE RE-TEST.** The Apple Watch screenshot was dated **July 27** but the workout previewed/saved under **today (2026-06-28)** with session `20260628-PM-01`. Root: the closeout path nulls the image (`closeoutAttachmentOnly`, `public/app.js`) and folds effort with the FORM date — the screenshot's own `.date` was never read; the `date_source` resolver lived only in `/api/complete-workout`, which this path bypasses. Fix (`public/app.js`, restricted trust-loop file, authorized by the FB item): new pure `resolveCloseoutWorkoutDate({manualDate, manualEntered, screenshotDate, today})` — an EXPLICITLY-typed manual date wins, else a confidently-parsed screenshot date (the vision parser only emits a strict `YYYY-MM-DD` when unambiguous, else null — validated client-side by `isConfidentScreenshotDate`), else today (flagged). Resolved in the closeout branch BEFORE `handleLogIt` re-submits, applied to `#log-date` AND the `session_id` (`generateSessionId(resolvedDate)`), so the rebuilt log rows, session_id, effort row, `pendingWrite` payload, and the preview banner all share the chosen date — they cannot drift. A new `logDateManuallyEntered` flag (set only on a real `input` event; `setDefaultDate` assigns `.value` programmatically and never trips it) distinguishes an explicit owner date from the default-today, and is CLEARED inside `setDefaultDate` so a one-time manual edit doesn't latch "manual" for the PWA's lifetime — the post-save reset calls `setDefaultDate`, so the next closeout screenshot date wins again (review #674 catch; `startOverWorkout` intentionally keeps the date and leaves the flag, since the field is unchanged there). The preview shows the chosen date + source via the shared `renderDateSourceNotice` ("Date from screenshot: 2026-07-27" / "Date (manual): …" / today-fallback warning) — reusing B5's exact copy (the complete-workout block was refactored onto the same helper). The preview/approve step IS the confirmation (no extra modal). Tests: 4 source-introspection cases in `test/unit.test.js` (priority order; closeout wiring applies date+session_id before re-submit; manual-entry flag; preview banner reads the write-payload date). Shell `v61→v62`. **Owner live re-test required** (criterion 1): July-dated screenshot at closeout → preview shows "Date from screenshot: …" → approve → Log_Cleaned + Effort saved under that date with a matching session_id. (Peak HR blank in the import is benign — `peakHR` is validated as optional, `index.js:589`.) No write-path/proof-field/schema/parser semantics changed; the preview→approve→write trust loop is intact.
  - **Deferred (RC1 review #673, non-blocking)** `[housekeeping]`: `log_rows_preview` now means "rows that WILL be written" on `/api/log-workout` (deduped) but still means "all parsed rows" on `/api/complete-workout` (where `rows_to_write` is the written set). The client reads the correct field per endpoint today (`public/app.js:5301` vs `:5378`), so it's harmless — but the same field name carrying different semantics across two write endpoints is a latent footgun for future client code. Align the two preview shapes (e.g. make complete-workout also expose the deduped preview set under the same name) when next touching that path; no behavior change needed now.
  - ✅ **RC3 — re-used screenshot hard-failed the PREVIEW with "Duplicate session"** `[trust-critical / UX]` (surfaced during the owner's RC2 live re-test, IMG_5063): re-importing the SAME dated screenshot produced the same screenshot-date `session_id`, already present in `Effort`, and `/api/complete-workout`'s duplicate-session guard (`index.js:3135`) returned **409 *before* the `test_mode` preview return (`:3206`)** — so the dry-run itself failed with a red "Preview failed: Duplicate session." (This was actually proof RC2 works — the session_id is now deterministic from the screenshot date.) Fix: gate that 409 on `!testMode` — the LIVE write still hard-stops a duplicate (never double-writes Effort), but the DRY-RUN continues to a normal preview that reports `duplicate_check.duplicate_session: true` (already in the response body). The client (`renderCompleteWorkoutPreview`) shows a graceful "✓ This workout is already saved — nothing new will be written" note and **disables approve** (the live write would 409 anyway), instead of a hard error. Tests: `test/api-smoke.test.js` (dry-run no longer 409s, flags duplicate_session; the existing live-409 test still passes) + `test/unit.test.js` (client note + approve-gate wiring). Shell `v63→v64`. No write-path/proof-field/schema/parser semantic change — the live duplicate guard is unchanged; only the preview degrades gracefully.
    - **Deferred (RC3 review #676, non-blocking)** `[housekeeping]`: on a duplicate-session dry-run that ALSO carries genuinely-new log rows, the preview can still show "N rows will be written" while the live write 409s on the effort-session duplicate before writing anything — a slight over-promise. Fully neutralized today (the "already saved" note + disabled approve mean the owner can't act on it, and a re-imported screenshot realistically carries identical, already-deduped rows). Fold into the RC1-deferred preview-write-parity alignment (make complete-workout's preview reflect that a duplicate effort-session blocks the whole write) when that path is next touched; no behavior change needed now.
  - ✅ **RC4 — a SAVED session restored as a "Session restored — 30 sets" ghost** `[trust-critical]` (surfaced during the live re-test, IMG_5125/5127): on reload the owner saw a restore banner for an already-saved 30-set workout, and every new save then collided on its already-saved `session_id` ("it thinks I'm in an old workout"). Root: the post-write session reset in the approve handler (`public/app.js`) cleared `sessionLog`/`sessionCompleted` only behind `if (pendingLastWrite)` — but `pendingLastWrite` is UNDO state (the appended log range) and is **null for screenshot / effort-only saves**, even though they DID save the session. So a saved screenshot session survived in memory; the next set-log / `visibilitychange` event re-ran `saveSessionSnapshot` (which persists whenever `sessionLog.length`), and the saved session came back on reload. Fix: make the reset UNGATED — a confirmed save (success OR duplicate; any mode) clears `sessionLog`, `sessionCompleted`, and ends the plan via `endPlannedSession()` (NOT a bare `activePlannedSession = null` — that would bypass Step 385's deload teardown; `endPlannedSession()` advances the `Deload_State` machine when `deloadWritten` before nulling the plan), so `saveSessionSnapshot`'s "nothing in progress" guard drops the snapshot and a saved workout can never restore. `lastWrite = pendingLastWrite` (undo state) is unchanged. Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (`saved-no-restore`: reset is ungated + clears the plan; `saveSessionSnapshot` drops the snapshot when clear) + 2 fixed-window approve-section assertions widened for the added comment. Shell `v65→v67`. No write-path/proof-field/schema/parser change. **(Owner model, confirmed 2026-06-28: a logged/saved workout must never show a restore banner; restore is only for an unsaved, lost in-progress session.)** **✅ Follow-up (PR 2 — interactive restore banner):** the banner is now interactive (`public/app.js` `renderResumeNotice` + `wireResumeNoticeGestures`): **TAP** brings the recovered workout into view (`restoreSessionToView` → `populateSetRows(buildRowsFromSessionLog())` + reveals the editable rows), and **SWIPE left → trash can → discard** (`discardRestoredSession`: clears `sessionLog`/`sessionCompleted`, ends the plan via `endPlannedSession()`, clears the `session_id` field + closeout state + snapshot, dispatches `atlas:session-reset`). The bare × dismiss is replaced by swipe-to-discard; the gesture aborts on a dominant vertical move so scrolling is preserved. This is the escape hatch for a stale/ghost restore (incl. a pre-fix ghost already in `localStorage` that RC4 can't retroactively erase). CSS: swipe-reveal layers in `styles.css`. Shell `v67→v69` (a follow-up commit gated the swipe reveal on the `horizontal` flag so a vertical-aborted diagonal can't reveal the trash — review #678). Tests: `test/unit.test.js` (`restore banner`: trash+discard+gesture+horizontal-gate wiring). **Needs an owner live re-test** (tap shows the workout; swipe reveals trash; trash discards and clears the ghost).
    - **Deferred (PR 2 review #678, non-blocking)** `[housekeeping / a11y]`: discard is **touch-only** — the trash sits behind the content (`z-index:0`) and is reached only by a horizontal swipe, so a non-touch (desktop) context has no way to reach discard and there's no `aria` action fallback. Fine for the single-owner iOS PWA today; if desktop/a11y ever matters, add a keyboard/click-reachable discard affordance (e.g. a visible discard button on hover/focus). No behavior change needed now.
  - ✅ **RC5 — multiple sessions per day: a fresh effort upload was forced to `…-01` and greyed out** `[trust-critical]` (surfaced live, IMG_5125 — owner: "it parsed good but it won't let me save it, it's greyed out"; debug dump showed no active/buffered session). **Owner requirement:** a user can log more than one session a day (gym workout, then a run/ride later) and the second should be a NEW session. **Owner-chosen design:** simple auto-increment ("it's just a spreadsheet — add a 2 to the end of the session id"), NOT content-matching. Root cause: for a complete-workout effort upload the client always fabricated a session_id — `sessionId = sessionIdInput.value.trim() || generateSessionId(date)` mints `…-01` when the field is empty, and the post-save reset (RC4) never cleared `#log-session-id`, so the just-written id lingered. A supplied `…-01` that already exists in `Effort` is flagged `duplicate_check.duplicate_session` → approve gated off → "greyed out." The server already auto-increments via `nextAvailableSessionId(date, existingEffortSessionIds)` **when no session_id is sent** — the client was just never sending blank. Fix (`public/app.js`, restricted trust-loop file, in scope per the owner's explicit multi-session request): (1) effort-only complete-workout uploads now send the RAW field value (`completeWorkoutSessionId = effortOnly ? explicitSessionId : sessionId`) — blank for a fresh upload → the server auto-increments to `…-02`; uploads carrying workout rows keep the resolved id so `Log_Cleaned` and `Effort` never disagree on session_id; (2) the effort-only branch captures the server-resolved id (`resolvedEffortData.session_id`) into `pendingWrite.sessionId` + the field so the live write reuses the SAME session the preview computed (mirrors the screenshot branch); (3) the post-write reset clears `#log-session-id` so a concluded save means the next upload starts blank = a new session. Approve-before-write, proof fields, `write_id` idempotency, and the explicit-id duplicate guard are all unchanged — a client-supplied id is still honored and still dup-checked. Tests: `test/api-smoke.test.js` (effort-only with no session_id auto-increments past an existing same-day `…-01` → `…-02`, Effort row + response agree, never flagged duplicate) + `test/unit.test.js` (submit gates the sent id on effortOnly + both branches submit it; effort-only captures the resolved id; saved session clears `#log-session-id`). Shell `v69→v70`. No schema / proof-field / write-path-semantics / parser change. **Needs an owner live re-test** (save a gym session, then upload a later effort-only screenshot/manual run → it saves as a second session `…-02`, not greyed out).
    - **Deferred — multi-session for a screenshot-WITH-sets fresh upload** `[correctness]`: the auto-increment is gated on `effortOnly` (no workout rows). A fresh screenshot/manual upload that ALSO carries logged sets still uses the client-resolved session_id, because the 12-col log rows are sent as arrays whose column-1 session_id is NOT re-stamped by the server (`logRowArrayToObject` ignores `topLevelSessionId`), so blanking the top-level id would desync `Log_Cleaned` (`…-01` rows) from `Effort` (`…-02`). To extend auto-increment to that path, either have the server stamp the resolved session_id onto all log rows, or send the rows as objects with a blank session_id. Low frequency (the owner's case is a later run = effort-only); file for a future multi-session sweep.
    - **Deferred — review-card "already saved" copy when approve is gated** `[polish]`: when a genuinely re-used (explicit-id duplicate) session disables Save, the in-thread review card note (`public/coach-conversation.js`) still reads "Nothing's saved yet · this is the only save," which contradicts the disabled Save. RC5 makes this rare (fresh uploads now auto-increment instead of colliding), so it only shows for a real re-uploaded already-saved session. Swap the note to an "already saved — nothing new to write" message when the card's Save is disabled due to `duplicate_session`. Display-only; no trust-loop/write-path change.
  - **(prior reconciliation note, now corrected):** the #612 trigger is present and the owner's preview-then-approve decision stands, but FB is NOT closed until RC1 (this PR) + RC2 (next PR) ship and the owner re-tests live.
  - **OWNER DECISION 2026-06-28: PREVIEW then approve** (approve-before-write trust loop preserved; the screenshot drives the SAME preview→approve→write as "done", it does NOT one-tap save). **(History) the PREVIEW trigger SHIPPED in PR #612** (`fix: completion-flow — screenshot opens preview + plan-complete stops nagging (G3+FB)`, commit `1ca813c`). The current `public/app.js` closeout guard (now ~`:4829`) stages the parsed screenshot effort and calls `await handleLogIt()` — driving the existing `runCloseout → preview → approve → write` — then `return`s; on re-entry the `closeoutAttachmentOnly` path folds the parsed effort into the normal log-workout preview as `manualEffort`. Status copy is "Effort read from screenshot — opening your preview to save." (no longer "Say done to preview…"). Approve-before-write is preserved (it opens a preview, never writes directly); `write_id` idempotency + the `lastWrite` "nothing new" guard backstop a later "done". Tests already lock it: `test/unit.test.js` "closeout screenshot: plan-complete attachment parses effort without workout ingestion" (asserts `await handleLogIt()`, the preview copy, the `return`, and no `complete-workout` call) + "closeout screenshot: done save uses normal log-workout preview and includes parsed effort". The shipped behavior MATCHES the owner's just-confirmed preview-then-approve decision, so **no further code is needed.** **Outstanding: owner live-test only** (owner check-in criterion 1) — upload a screenshot at closeout → preview shows the sets + effort → approve writes once → a later "done" is a no-op. _Original analysis retained for the record:_ After the plan completes, the prompt says "Say 'done' or take a screenshot to save" (`coach-conversation.js:1168–1169`), but uploading the effort screenshot only **stages** the effort and re-prompts for "done" — it does not complete. **Root:** "done" and screenshot ingest are **separate handlers** in `public/app.js` — `looksLikeLogIt` (:1650) → `handleLogIt` (:3905) → `runCloseout` (:3916) drives preview→approve→write, while the screenshot branch (:4298–4309, gated on `isPlanCloseoutAwaitingSave()` :3420) stashes `closeoutScreenshotFile`, reads effort, sets "Effort read from screenshot. Say done to preview…" (:4304) and **`return`s**. Fix: after staging the screenshot effort, invoke the existing `handleLogIt()` so the upload drives the **same** closeout. **Reuses the existing save path (no new write path); `write_id` idempotency + the `lastWrite` "nothing new" guard (:3935) backstop double-save** — but this touches the completion trigger, so it needs care + a required live-test card (upload → preview with effort → approve writes once → later "done" is a no-op). **Owner decision needed:** screenshot → **preview** (approve-before-write preserved, recommended) vs **one-tap save** (a trust-loop change). **Independent of FA.** Opus; live-test card required.
- **FC = G3 (carry-over, not re-filed):** wrong "Next" / premature "say done" is the next-up/closeout **gating** in `handleSetLogged` (~:1114–1131) — a distinct code root from FA (render order) and FB (screenshot trigger); FB and G3 are in the completion-flow *family* but not the same code. See the existing G3 item.
- **Open questions for Dale:** (1) FB intent — screenshot goes to preview (recommended) or completes the save in one step (trust-loop change)? (2) FA — on a stacked entry with a substitution, should the sub acknowledgement attach per-lift or stay one line?

## P0 — Active Workout State Unification / Adaptive Workout State — TOP PRIORITY (owner-approved lane, 2026-06-24)

`[trust-critical]` A real Coach's Pick gym session (v39) exposed that Atlas has **no single authoritative active-workout object**: after mid-session changes the coach conversation, next-up router, composer prefill, workout queue, preview/save, and completion logic drift apart (stale composer/next-up after a swap, corrected identity understood in chat but not on the card, inserted accessories only buffered, preview fails on a heavily-modified session, coach claims "no session plan" mid-session). **Required invariant: exactly one authoritative ActiveSession; every consumer derives from it; accepted modifications MUTATE it deterministically (the LLM explains, the app state owns).** Full diagnosis + target design + acceptance→PR map: [`docs/ACTIVE_SESSION_STATE_DIAGNOSIS.md`](./docs/ACTIVE_SESSION_STATE_DIAGNOSIS.md). Owner granted the trust-critical lane (stop only for live-test/schema/destructive/credentials/governance). Slices: **✅ PR 1 — diagnosis + failing repro tests (this PR)**; **✅ PR 2 — active session wiring (PR #618 + follow-up `claude/p0-pr2-review-fixes`)** — replaced stale `exercises[index]` reads in `app.js` with canonical session (`currentPlannedExercise()` helper + `firstUnloggedPlannedLift()` in `curName()`); fixed `checkAndSuggestSubstitute`, `plan_exercises` payload, `advancePlannedSession` skip-vs-increment branch, and `renderActiveSessionBanner` null guard. Follow-up: (a) index fallback in `currentPlannedExercise()` when `window.activeSession` absent, (b) `pendingSubstitution` skip guard so Step 379 protection holds with canonical-session reads. `[housekeeping]` **Behavioral test gap (reviewer note PR #618):** `test/activeSessionWiring.test.js` is source-introspection only; a behavioral test that drives `checkAndSuggestSubstitute` with a declared swap and asserts the second call targets the next exercise (not the taken lift) would harden against regression — but `checkAndSuggestSubstitute` is DOM-bound (`api()`, event dispatch) and has no existing behavioral harness. File when the substitute check path next gets a harness. **✅ PR 3 — regenerate full replacement prescription incl. warm-up/ramp** (PR #620, merged 2026-06-26): `POST /api/suggest-substitute` now returns a full next-set prescription (weight/reps/sets) for the substitute exercise by calling `recommendNextSet` via `generateLiftCode` (the correct Sheets `lift_code` namespace) — identical to the prescription path used for `recommendTargetForLift`. Bug: initial build used `resolveExercise` (KB `exercise_id` namespace), so `recommendNextSet` always returned null. Fix: `generateLiftCode(rec.recommendation)` → correct code → `recommendNextSet` → prescription fields populated. Tests: AC3 hardened to assert positive number fields (`nt.weight`, `nt.reps`, `nt.sets`). **✅ PR 4 — exercise-identity correction** (AC7, this PR): "sorry that was lat pulldown" relabels the just-logged lift in `sessionLog[]` (trailing contiguous run) + reconciles `sessionCompleted[]` deterministically — no LLM required. Classifier (`public/identityCorrection.js`), canonical operation (`public/activeSession.js::correctIdentity`), app wiring (`public/app.js::tryApplyIdentityCorrection`), coach narration (`public/coach-conversation.js::atlas:identity-corrected` listener) all pre-built. New behavioral harness: `test/identityCorrectionWiring.test.js` (12 tests) drives `tryApplyIdentityCorrection` end-to-end — relabel, sessionCompleted reconciliation, trailing-run-only scope, stale-name absence, event dispatch, catalog guard, no-write-path introspection. 2815 pass / 0 fail. **✅ PR 5 — insert/finisher handling + recap/save** (AC8/9/10, this PR): `insertExercise`, `getCanonicalSession()` insert wiring, `canonicalSessionRecap()`, and `hasLoggedWork()` guard all pre-built. New behavioral harness: `test/insertFinisherWiring.test.js` (10 tests) drives `getCanonicalSession()` and `canonicalSessionRecap()` end-to-end — off-plan exercise gets `source:'inserted'`, planned exercises stay non-inserted, recap includes inserted finishers, all-skipped session returns null, fully-modified recap splits completed/remaining correctly, freestyle session, multiple off-plan inserts, write-path introspection. 2825 pass / 0 fail. **✅ PR 6 — barbell loadability guard (plate inventory)** (AC12, this PR): all components pre-built. Pure math in `services/barbellLoadability.js` (`roundToLoadableBarbell`, `isLoadableBarbell`); KB gate + snap in `services/barbellLoadabilitySurface.js` (`isBarbellExercise`, `applyBarbellLoadability`, `applyBarbellLoadabilityToExercises`); wired at `index.js:2204` (`/api/plan/intent-recommendation`); drawer note rendered at `public/app.js:1783`. Tests: `test/barbellLoadability.test.js` (7 tests) + `test/barbellLoadabilitySurface.test.js` (6 tests) + structural wiring in `test/unit.test.js`. 2825 pass / 0 fail. Discovered follow-up filed below: `suggest-substitute` prescription weight (`next_target.weight`) is not snapped — the loadability guard only runs on the intent-recommendation surface, not on substitute prescriptions. **✅ PR 7 — end-to-end regression (AC10 full path, this PR):** new behavioral harness `test/activeSessionE2E.test.js` (13 tests) drives all five mutation operations in sequence — substitute (DL→Back Squat), markCompleted, identity correction (Pull-Ups→Lat Pulldown), insertExercise×2 + markCompleted — on the same session object (Layer 1 / pure `activeSession.js`), then verifies `getCanonicalSession()` and `canonicalSessionRecap()` with the post-mutation plan+log state the real app produces (Layer 2 / app.js harness). Proves: no phantom exercises, no duplicate entries; off-plan inserts appear as `source:'inserted'`; corrected log name treated as off-plan insert (planned slot stays in remaining, never collapsed into a phantom completed entry); write-path source-introspection guards pass. 2838 pass / 0 fail. **P0 lane complete (all 7 PRs shipped).** After P0: roadmap proposal for Athlete DNA / Training Preference Intelligence (learn likely next exercises, finishers, avoided lifts, common subs from history) — do not let it distract from the active roadmap.

- 🔴 **ROOT CAUSE confirmed (v51 retest 2026-06-25) — Gemini FREE-TIER QUOTA EXHAUSTED (429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED)** `[owner-action: billing]`: the surfaced inner error is `429 ... Quota exceeded for metric generativelanguage.googleapis.com/generate_content_free_tier_requests, limit: 20, model: gemini-2.5-flash-lite`. SINGLE root of BOTH (a) the coach feeling robotic (every Gemini call 429s → deterministic-template fallback) AND (b) the screenshot save failing ("Failed to complete workout ingestion" — the screenshot vision parse is also a Gemini call → 429 → 500). **Owner fix (credentials/billing, owner-reserved): enable billing / upgrade GEMINI_API_KEY to a paid tier (or raise quota).** CODE follow-up — ✅ **shipped** (screenshot graceful-degrade): `/api/complete-workout` now wraps `parseWorkoutScreenshot` in its own try/catch — a vision parse failure (429/timeout) no longer 500s the whole save. With logged sets present, the sets are saved WITHOUT effort (blank effort row keeps session linkage + duplicate protection), `effort_source:'screenshot_unreadable'` + `screenshot_unreadable:true` are returned, and the owner-facing warning "I couldn't read effort from the screenshot. I can still save the workout without effort data." is surfaced. An effort-only request (no rows) with a failed parse returns an honest **422** (nothing left to save), never a 500, and never appends. Tests in `test/api-smoke.test.js`. **UPDATE (2026-06-26): owner enabled the PAID Gemini API** — the durable quota cure for the coach-robotic / "couldn't reach the coach" side. A free-tier-429 degrade was still seen intermittently during the FA/LT-005 run before billing; should be resolved now. The provider-failover backlog item remains the resilience follow-up (paid Gemini can still 503 under load), but the free-tier-429 root cause is addressed.
- **"Preview failed: Failed to complete workout ingestion" — diagnosis (Sub-PR 2b)** `[correctness]`: this string comes ONLY from the outer catch of `/api/complete-workout` (`index.js:3198`) — the **screenshot / Apple-Watch-effort** save path; the manual-text path (`/api/log-workout`) never emits it. In **preview mode** (`test_mode=true`) the entire `if (!testMode)` write block is skipped, so the only unguarded throw points that reach 3198 are `resolveWorkoutDate` (2893), `buildEffortRowFromParsedMetrics` (2952), and `getLogCompositeKeys` (2964, a Sheets read). The enrichment of modified-session rows is wrapped in its own try that returns a specific **400 "Log rows validation/enrichment failed"**, NOT 3198. **Conclusion: the diagnosis doc's "heavily-modified session → ingestion failure" link is not substantiated in code** — a modified session does not deterministically cause this 500; the most likely real cause is a transient Sheets read (`getLogCompositeKeys`/effort-id fetch) or an effort-metric edge during the closeout-with-screenshot save. **Action: capture the actual `error.message` in the next live retest** (the response includes it as `error` outside production) before any server change; the effort/complete-workout write path is owner-gated and out of the P0 frontend wiring scope. Not fixed in 2b (no evidence of a deterministic frontend cause).
- **Warmup-ramp re-derivation after the barbell snap (PR #572 review)** `[polish]`: the AC12 loadability snap (Sub-PR 2c) runs on the recommendation surface AFTER any warmup ramp is built — so a *structured-session* path that surfaces `warmup_sets` (built by `buildWarmupRamp` off the pre-snap `next_target.weight`) would show a ramp computed from e.g. 116 while the snapped top set reads 115. Cosmetic today (ramps are `roundLoad`'d 50/70/85% so the one-plate delta is invisible) and **not applicable to the surface 2c actually touches** (`/api/plan/intent-recommendation` exercises carry no `warmup_sets`). If a future slice surfaces warmup weights alongside the snapped top set, re-snap the ramp after loadability or document the ordering. Also note (PR #572 review, safe edge): `isBarbellExercise` uses any `resolveExercise` record regardless of the `confident` flag — harmless because the snap only fires on `category==='barbell'`/`equipment includes 'barbell'` of canonical recommendation names; tighten with a confidence gate only if a low-confidence fuzzy alias is ever observed snapping a non-barbell lift.

## Planning philosophy — Blind Spots are a Stimulus Modifier, not the workout (owner directive 2026-06-25)

✅ **Shipped.** Live testing exposed that "Fix Blind Spots" Coach's Pick produced a *narrow* session (e.g. Deadlift + Side Bend + Single-Leg Leg Curl) because `fix_blind_spots` filtered the exercise pool to ONLY the overdue patterns. Owner-defined planning hierarchy: **(1) user goal → (2) recovery/readiness → (3) rotation/split → (4) session structure → (5) blind spots → (6) exercise selection → (7) load.** Blind spots must MODIFY an otherwise-complete session, not replace it. Fix: `fix_blind_spots` now calls `buildIntentSession({ patterns: [all major patterns], priorityPatterns: freshIds })` — a FULL structured session across push/pull/lower/hinge/core that EMPHASIZES the overdue patterns (anchor + early fill via the new `priorityPatterns` stable-sort in `services/sessionBuilder.js`), then the existing readiness density caps + learned session-volume profile (`structureSession`/`capSessionToProfile`) right-size it to the athlete's habitual session length. `recovery_pump`/`short_session`/`test_progress` remain intentionally abbreviated (explicit focused modes).
- **Deferred follow-ups** `[correctness]`: (1) **Explicit athlete-profile/preference layer** — the owner wants Coach's Pick to respect a recurring profile (heavy compound first, 1-2 primary compounds, push+pull balance, accessories, core, optional arms/finishers, ~60-75 min). Today this is *implicit* via `orderByRole` + the learned `sessionVolumeProfile` (median size + 75th-pct caps); there is no explicit preferred-lift / split / rotation model. Promote to a real "Athlete DNA / Training Preference Intelligence" track (already noted under P0) if the implicit shape proves insufficient in live use. (2) **Full-session-by-default audit** — ✅ partially locked: a regression test (`test/analytics-edge.test.js`) now asserts `build_muscle`/`balanced`/`fix_blind_spots` build a full multi-pattern session (≥4 exercises, ≥3 muscle groups) for a rich history, and the abbreviated intents stay small. **(2a) OPEN — OWNER COACHING DECISION `[correctness]` — `build_strength` is an UPPER-ONLY (push+pull) day by design for a non-strength goal** (probe 2026-06-25: rec=build_strength → 3 lifts [Bench, OHP, Row] while build_muscle/balanced/fix_blind_spots → 5). **Corrected root cause** (an earlier note wrongly blamed the `backfillToProfileTarget` rested-only fill — verified that's a no-op here because nothing was trimmed): the real cause is `services/analytics.js:1537` — `strengthPatterns = (goal === 'strength' && isFresh('lower')) ? ['lower','hinge','push','pull'] : ['push','pull']`. For the default (non-strength) goal the strength day is **deliberately push+pull only** (legs added only when the goal is explicitly `strength` AND legs are fresh). So a 3-lift upper day is intentional, not a backfill bug. This **conflicts with the owner's "full session by default + respect athlete profile" directive** (the athlete's profile is push+pull+legs+accessories+core, ~60-75 min). **Decision needed (coaching composition, owner-reserved despite autonomy because my minimal-fix premise already failed once):** should the DEFAULT strength day be broadened toward the athlete's full profile (e.g. include a lower/hinge compound + core, sized by `capSessionToProfile`), or stay upper-focused by design? If broadened: smallest change is widening the default `strengthPatterns` to include `lower`/`hinge`/`core` (still readiness-capped + profile-sized + readiness-dosed). **✅ RESOLVED 2026-06-28 (Current-State Verification Gate): the broadening was ALREADY SHIPPED.** The push+pull-only default this note described no longer exists — `services/analytics.js` now defaults to `strengthPatterns = upperOnly ? ['push','pull'] : ['lower','hinge','push','pull']` (the "upperOnly flag" PR, backlog "Wire the explicit upper-only request" item), with dedicated regression tests at `test/analytics-edge.test.js` ("build_strength is FULL-PROFILE by default", "upper-only only on explicit intent", "preserves progression targets"). So the default strength day is already a whole-body session (lower + hinge + push + pull), not a 3-lift upper day. **Owner decision 2026-06-28: ACCEPT as-is** — that broadening satisfies "full profile" for build_strength; do NOT force-prescribe core.
  - **Deferred product note — core never surfaces in any prescribed session** `[correctness, cross-intent]` `product-decision`: investigating the build_strength "+core" gap revealed core is dropped for **every** training intent, not just build_strength. Root: `core` classifies as an `accessory` (`classifyLiftRole`), and `capSessionToProfile` (`services/sessionBuilder.js:376`) trims the lowest-priority accessories to fit the learned session size — so a logged core lift (e.g. Cable Crunch) is capped out of `build_strength` AND `build_muscle`/`balanced`/`fix_blind_spots` alike (all four list `core` in their pattern set, none surface it for a realistic fixture; verified by probe). Adding `core` to a pattern list is therefore a **behavioral no-op**. **Owner direction 2026-06-28: do NOT guarantee a core slot / do NOT force-prescribe core across intents just to satisfy a checklist.** Core must still (a) *count correctly when logged* (volume/readiness — already true) and (b) *surface when genuinely undertrained or relevant* (e.g. a core blind-spot signal). Promote to a real change only if live use shows core is being neglected because it never gets prescribed when it should — at which point the fix is a targeted "reserve a core slot when core readiness is overdue" signal inside session sizing, NOT a blanket forced slot. Engine-only when built; no write/schema/trust-loop impact.

## Trust-Critical Coach Interaction Layer — TOP PRIORITY (diagnosis-first)

`[trust-critical]` Live testing (2026-06-20) surfaced that Atlas loses active-session context and answers educational questions when the lifter is clearly asking about the active workout ("What am I doing next?", "Weight? Reps? RIR?", "How much am I lifting, how many reps, how many sets?"). Expected: answer from the active-session prescription. Actual: generic RIR/rep-range/volume explanations. **Goal: Atlas must always prioritize active workout context over generic fitness knowledge.** Full diagnosis: [`docs/COACH_INTERACTION_TRUST_INVESTIGATION.md`](./docs/COACH_INTERACTION_TRUST_INVESTIGATION.md); priority queue: [`docs/ACTIVE_ROADMAP.md`](./docs/ACTIVE_ROADMAP.md). **P0 shipped (#442, #446); coach-down engine fallback shipped (#449); P2 (#440) shipped/merged; P1 needs an owner live repro; P3/P4 not started.**

- ✅ **P0 — Active Session Context Integrity** — *shipped (PR #442, owner-approved/merged).* Root cause: `getChatReply` routed **SME-first** (`/api/coach/ask`, no session context) before the session-aware coach, short-circuiting on any card match — so mid-workout shorthand (`RIR`→`rir_rpe`, `how many reps`→`rep_ranges`, `how many sets`→`training_volume`) got generic education. Fix (routing only): new `public/sessionQuestion.js::isSessionStateQuestion` + a gate in `getChatReply` that skips the SME and routes to `/api/coach/chat` first **only** when an active workout exists (`current_plan`/`current_preview`/`plan_completed`) AND the message is session-shaped; education/ambiguous keep SME-first. No LLM/prompt, write-path, schema, trust-loop, or parser change. Tests: `test/sessionQuestion.test.js` + SW cache-guard.
  - **Deferred (complementary, not built — owner "no broad AI prompt changes"):** `buildChatSystemPrompt` "answer current-exercise prescription from session state first" rule `[polish]`. The LLM answer currently relies on the `current_plan`/`current_preview` context the coach already receives. Promote if the owner's live spot-check shows prescription answers drifting.
  - **Deferred (review note):** classifier patterns `/\binstead\b/` and `/\bhow much\b/` are broad; during an active workout an education-flavored question with those tokens routes to the (read-only, grounded) session coach `[polish]`. Safe; tighten only if it misroutes real education questions in live use.
- ✅ **Coach-down deterministic engine fallback** *(shipped #449)*: with routing fixed, session shorthand reached `/api/coach/chat` but dead-ended at "Coach is unavailable" when Gemini was down/throttled (the log path degrades to engine templates; chat did not). New `services/sessionQuestionAnswer.js` answers `RIR?/reps?/sets?/how much?` from `recommendNextSet`/plan-preview targets when the LLM is unavailable; chat client timeout 9s→15s so the fallback reaches the lifter; proposals preserved on empty prose; engine consulted when context lacks the asked attribute.
  - **Deferred (review note, #449)** `[polish]`: `ATTR_PATTERNS` in `sessionQuestionAnswer` is broad — on the **Gemini-down path only**, an off-topic question containing `how much`/`sets`/`reps` (e.g. "how much should I sleep?") can resolve a lift from session context and answer with that lift's target. Graceful-degradation tradeoff; only fires when the LLM is unavailable. Gate on a question-shape check before resolving from context **only if false positives surface in live testing** (note: `isSessionStateQuestion` alone won't catch "how much should I sleep?").
  - **Open — Gemini coach reliability** `[correctness]`: live testing (2026-06-20) showed `/api/coach/chat` (and set-reaction) intermittently failing — likely Gemini rate-limiting/quota during heavy testing or latency hitting the 8s server timeout. The #449 fallback prevents dead-ends, but the underlying LLM flakiness is worth confirming in Render logs (429s) and possibly raising quota / tuning timeouts. **Deferred** unless "Coach unavailable" persists after #449.
  - ✅ **Follow-up (#449 live re-test) — active-session bare shorthand routing + RIR/RPE wording** *(shipped #452, 2026-06-21).* Bare shorthand ("RIR?", "Reps?", "How much?", "How many sets?") now answers from the **current lift** (active preview wins, else single planned lift) whether or not Gemini is up; ambiguous → "For which lift — A or B?"; no active context → SME education (now correctly worded — RIR = reps in reserve, RIR 2 ≈ 2 good reps left, RIR 0 = none; no RPE conflation / 1-10 scale). New `isBareSessionShorthand`/`answerBareShorthand` + a pre-empt in `/api/coach/chat`. Read-only; no write/schema/parser/recommendation-math change.
    - ✅ **Resolved (#452 follow-up, shipped #536)** `[polish]`: during an active **preview** of a lift NOT in `current_plan`, preview rows carry `sets: null`, so a bare "How many sets?" couldn't be answered from context and fell through to the LLM. Fixed in PR #536: the `/api/coach/chat` bare pre-empt now, when the context-only `answerBareShorthand` returns null **and** the message is bare shorthand **and** an active session exists (`hasActiveSessionContext`), reads the log once and retries with `resolveTarget: (name)=>recommendTargetForLift(name, allLog)` — parity with the named-lift fallback path. Read-only; no write/schema/parser/coach-wording change. api-smoke regression proves the previewed unplanned lift gets the engine set count (Gemini up) and a guard proves a bare shorthand with no active session does not engine-fill.
- **P1 — Coach Signal Visibility Audit** — *Current-state verdict: SERVER-SIDE PASS / LIVE FRONTEND PENDING (2026-06-21).* Substitution-quality signal (`scoreSubstitutionQuality`, wired PR #439) only fires when a prescribed-vs-logged pair reaches `buildSubstitutionPreviews` (`prescribedList`) — driven by explicit swap phrasing / attached `plan_exercises`, NOT by silently comparing logged lifts to the active plan. The earlier "not visible" used *passive conversational* phrasing ("I'm going to substitute deadlift with rdl") routed to `/api/coach/chat`, so the log-time path never ran.
  - **Deterministic evidence (2026-06-21, headless repro):** parser extracts `prescribed:[{exercise:"Deadlift", reason:"platform busy"}]` from "Deadlift skipped — platform busy."; "RDL 225 8/2" parses cleanly (canonical RDL, 3 sets); `classifySubstitution(Deadlift→RDL)` → **preserved / approve** (both hinge, 67% overlap); `scoreSubstitutionQuality(Deadlift,RDL)` → **excellent** (`same_pattern_same_cost`); the dry-run backend path (`/api/log-workout` with `prescribed`) already has api-smoke tests for `substitutions[]` carrying classification/decision/quality. **Only the live frontend payload/wording path is unproven.**
  - **Owner live test script:** from an active plan prescribing Deadlift, in the normal log entry enter `Deadlift skipped — platform busy. RDL 225 8/2 x3`. Expected: preview includes the RDL sets; recognizes Deadlift→RDL as preserved/excellent; because it's a *good* swap the signal may be brief/quiet (a short "intent preserved" / "same hinge" line = working as designed). **If no substitution indication appears at all**, trace the frontend payload wiring from active plan / prescribed exercise into the dry-run payload (`lastPrescribed → prescribed/logged_exercise` in `public/app.js`).
  - **Do not code P1 yet.** Do not build around a signal that may be intentionally quiet — wait for the owner live app-test to decide quiet-vs-absent.
  - **Open — RDL "stalled at 185 for 3 sessions" trust check** `[trust-critical]`: during live testing the coach claimed RDL stalled at 185×3 sessions, which the owner believes is inconsistent with actual history. Source = the `stalls` array (`detectStalls`) the coach words. Needs the owner's RDL log rows to disambiguate: if `detectStalls` emitted RDL@185/3 → identity/detection bug; if not → LLM fabricated it (grounding violation). Stop-and-report until data is available.
- ◐ **P3 — Coach Brevity Pass** — Conclusion first, Reason second, Details only when asked (e.g. "Hold 116. You're right on target."). *In progress: conclusion-first ordering shipped into the coach voice.* Added a `CONCLUSION FIRST` rule to all three LLM voices (set-reaction / plan / chat) in `services/coach.js` + a 7th quality ("Conclusion first") in `docs/COACHING_NOTE_VOICE.md`; presentation-order only (never changes what is answered or any grounding rule). Source-introspection tests in `test/coachPromptRules.test.js`. PM authority under Escalation Policy v3 (coach wording). Owner live spot-check recommended.
  - **Deferred (P3 follow-ups)** `[polish]`: (1) flip the **deterministic stimulus-governor reason strings** (`services/stimulusGovernor.js` ~lines 75/78/94/97) to verdict-first — currently lead with the RIR/CNS reason before the hold/add call (these are not currently text-asserted in tests, and may be unwired/internal — confirm they reach a user surface before flipping). (2) the `public/coachVoiceTemplates.js` next-move advisory lines (~71–76) open with a generic "Heads up —" label rather than the action verdict; optional conclusion-first reword. Build only as a separate tiny PR; most other deterministic templated copy is already conclusion-first.
