## Atlas voice & interaction — TOP PRIORITY

The spearhead: the engine already emits verdicts (PR 3.3 ✅) and detects swaps (PR 3.5 ✅). The next build is wording those facts and giving the user control over how much Atlas says. Credibility floor (AC8) must be solid before voice ships.

- **NEW — Coaching Depth / Verbosity setting** _(owner-confirmed direction, future enhancement)_: a user-chosen dial for how much Atlas *explains*, not what it decides. Tiers: **Quiet** (minimal reaction, near engine-only) → **Standard** (current terse spec — ≤4 sentences, default to quiet) → **Coach** (more reasoning/training rationale) → **Deep Coach** (fuller coaching moments, training context, decision explanations). The underlying engine verdicts, rules, and safety constraints are **identical across tiers** — only the amount of explanation changes; never the numbers. Anchors the settings panel's chattiness slider below. Origin: the longer discursive style in PR #322's voice-validation examples is good *judgment* but too long for the Standard merge target; it becomes the Coach/Deep Coach tiers rather than the default. NOTE: some validation examples (recovery-violation rule, plateau/neutral verdict, "far above" magnitude tiers) assume engine signals PR 3.3 does **not** emit — those are engine work (the voice layer must never invent verdicts/rules), tracked separately if pursued. Voice-validation corpus (10 owner-approved ideal-session examples + 20 real-world-constraint scenarios with Atlas responses) lives in [`docs/COACH_VOICE_VALIDATION.md`](./docs/COACH_VOICE_VALIDATION.md) — the tonal/judgment reference for these tiers.
- **Engine prerequisites for the Coach / Deep Coach tiers** _(the verbose voice can only WORD signals the engine emits — these unlock the Set B scenarios in `docs/COACH_VOICE_VALIDATION.md`)_. Each is deterministic engine/data work, pure (no Sheets, no LLM); the voice layer never derives them:
  - **Substitution + intent classification** — extend `detectSwap` (PR 3.5, name-match only) to compare a logged lift against the prescribed one by movement pattern + muscle (`services/movementPattern.js` + `services/muscleCoverage.js`) and classify the swap as **preserved / changed / abandoned**. Drives B1/B2/B4 (preserved) vs B10/B12/B20 (changed/abandoned). This is the single biggest unlock. Spec: [`docs/SUBSTITUTION_SPEC.md`](./docs/SUBSTITUTION_SPEC.md) (PR 1 ✅ — spec only, ephemeral computed fact, no schema/tab/write-path changes; classifier code follows in later PRs).
    - ✅ **WIRING GAP — skip-notation path** _(resolved [#338](https://github.com/tc5v5s64ym-sketch/atlas-workout-updater/pull/338))_: parser now extracts `prescribed: [{exercise, reason}]` from inline "X skipped - reason." sentences; frontend passes it through to the `/api/log-workout` dry-run payload so `buildSubstitutionPreviews` fires.
    - ✅ **`reason` field wired downstream** _(resolved [#339](https://github.com/tc5v5s64ym-sketch/atlas-workout-updater/pull/339))_: `reason` now flows from parser → frontend payload → `buildSubstitutionPreviews` → classification result. Equipment-keyword reasons (platform/rack/machine/unavailable/etc.) synthesize a transient constraint so `classifySubstitution` can upgrade to `equipment_constraint_honored`. Coach `sanitizeSubstitution` whitelist includes `reason`; coach prompt allows incorporating the stated reason; templated fallback appends "Reason: …" when present. **Remaining gaps (deferred):**
      - **Prose substitution paths not yet wired:** `extractSkipNotes` only fires for explicit `. `‑delimited "X skipped ..." sentences. Multi-paragraph prose substitutions ("swapping back squats for leg press") and newline-separated skip+sets are handled by `extractSetParagraphs` (which strips the prose paragraph) but never surface a `prescribed` field — no wiring yet for those paths. Also: `findExerciseInText` matches any catalog exercise anywhere in the lead text, so prose like "Bench felt great so I skipped deadlift" extracts **Bench** (not deadlift) — wrong prescribed, but dry-run only and `detectSwap` drops identical prescribed/logged pairs. A proper fix requires anchoring the match to the start of the lead sentence, which is the documented "X skipped …" format — deferred with the broader prose-path work.
      - ✅ **Plan-prescribed wiring** _(resolved PR 341)_: `services/planMatcher.js` → `inferPrescribedPairs` infers substitution pairs from `activePlannedSession` when the user didn't write an explicit skip sentence. `index.js` test_mode block accepts `plan_exercises` from the payload and merges inferred pairs into `prescribedList` before calling `buildSubstitutionPreviews`. `public/app.js` attaches `plan_exercises` from `activePlannedSession.exercises` to both the session preview payload and the mid-session substitution check.
      - ✅ **`planMatcher` identity is name-only — lift_code guard** — fixed. `inferPrescribedPairs` now runs a Step 1b `lift_code` identity pass after the exact-name pass: equal non-null `lift_code` (case-insensitive) marks the planned lift fulfilled even when names differ (plan `"Bench Press"` vs logged `"Barbell Bench Press"`), so Step 2 no longer mislabels the same lift as a substitution. Differing codes / one-sided codes fall through to the existing name/pattern logic unchanged. Dry-run only — no data risk. 5 golden tests added; full suite green.
      - **Full-session preview path: partial-log sends the full plan — same broad-region mis-attribution risk as mid-session:** `public/app.js` session-preview path (≈line 3325) attaches the entire `activePlannedSession.exercises` list as `plan_exercises`. If the user submits a partial log (one exercise done so far while a full plan is active), `inferPrescribedPairs` sees all planned lifts competing for that one logged exercise — the broad-region fallback can claim it for the wrong planned lift (same ambiguity the mid-session fix resolved by sending only the current step). Dry-run only — no data risk; only narration/substitution-preview is affected. Deferred alongside the other attribution edge cases; symmetry fix would send only the exercises the user has reached so far, which requires the frontend to know the logged count relative to the plan index.
      - ✅ **`scoreSubstitutionQuality` scores same-pattern isolation→isolation as `excellent` regardless of muscle group (PR 342 deferred)** — fixed. `computePrimaryMuscleOverlap` is now exported from `substitutionIntent.js` and used in the isolation branch: when a same-pattern pair is isolation→isolation (both LOW cost) and both primary-muscle sets are known, the grade is by primary-muscle overlap — `≥ OVERLAP_THRESHOLD` keeps `excellent` (`same_pattern_same_muscle`), partial → `acceptable` (`isolation_partial_muscle_overlap`), zero → `poor` (`isolation_different_muscle`). Leg Curl → Leg Extension (hamstrings vs quads) now scores `poor`. Missing muscle data on either side falls through conservatively to `same_pattern_same_cost` (no guessed downgrade). 5 golden tests added; full suite green.
        - ✅ **Surfaced into the coach voice (was dark)** — `scoreSubstitutionQuality`'s tier is now attached in `buildSubstitutionPreviews` (`index.js`) → `sub.quality`, bounded by `sanitizeSubstitution` (enum `excellent|acceptable|poor|unknown`, else null), and worded by the reaction + verdict-reaction prompts per owner-approved rules: **poor → volunteer** what muscle/intent the swap missed; acceptable → one-line tradeoff; excellent → brief acknowledgement; and **never call a swap good / say it "counts" unless the engine tier is excellent/acceptable** (absent/unknown → no quality judgement). Owner-approved wordings in `COACH_VOICE_VALIDATION.md`. 3 coach tests + the existing suite (2086) green.
        - ✅ **muscle-data gap (triceps) — closed.** Added a `triceps_isolation` entry to `muscleCoverage.js` (pushdown/pressdown/skullcrusher/tricep extension → `['triceps']`), so Bicep Curl → Tricep Pushdown now scores `poor` (different muscle) and triceps work counts toward triceps coverage everywhere. Owner-approved the downstream deload behavior (a stalling triceps isolation may now be downgraded when pressing already covers triceps). Two guard tests re-pointed to still-unmapped vehicles (Calf Raise; Reverse Fly→Lateral Raise). Full suite green (2052). **Remaining gaps (still unmapped isolations):** calves (Calf Raise), rear delts (Reverse Fly / Rear Delt Fly) — fill when a pair needs them; same pure-data pattern.
        - ✅ **Resolved (decision, PR #439) — `computePrimaryMuscleOverlap` directionality is the correct semantics, consciously accepted.** It returns the fraction of *prescribed* primaries covered by the *logged* set — which is exactly the question substitution quality asks: "did what you actually did cover what was planned?" The direction is fixed by the real scenario (prescribed = the plan, logged = what was performed); the "reverse" pairing never occurs in use. So the asymmetry is not a bug and a symmetric overlap (Jaccard) would be *wrong* here (it would dilute "logged covers prescribed" with prescribed-not-in-logged). Gate from #431 closed: the tier is now surfaced (PR #439) with this intent documented; no code change needed.
      - **Multi-swap: `loggedExercise` is always `logRows[0]` for all prescribed pairs** (pre-existing, carried into PR #340). A single input with two skip sentences ("Squat skipped - rack busy. Bench skipped - station full. Deadlift 315 5/2") would pair both skip notes to Deadlift. Fixing requires iterating prescribed pairs against their own matched logged row (closest exercise match in the same input), rather than pinning all to the first. Deferred: multi-skip inputs are uncommon; fixing is a `buildSubstitutionPreviews` pairing-logic change.
  - **Pre-session pain / fatigue / constraint intake** — a forward-looking, opt-in signal (pain location, sleep/fatigue, time budget, equipment limits) the planner reads to redirect/modify and the voice words. Distinct from the existing post-hoc `pain_flag` (notes-scan). Drives B3/B13/B17. Must honor the Someday "readiness — non-intrusive / never interrogate" guardrail.
  - ✅ **Unprogrammed-extra-work detection** — engine built: `services/extraWorkDetector.js::detectExtraWork(prescribed, logged)` flags `extra_sets` (a planned lift logged for more sets than its `target_sets`) and `extra_exercises` (logged but never prescribed), matching by lift_code then name. Pure/deterministic, no I/O/LLM/write. 13 golden tests. Drives B6.
    - ✅ **Owner-approved Standard-tier wording (voice reference)** — `docs/COACH_VOICE_VALIDATION.md` "Extra-work voice" section (owner-approved 2026-06-20, Owner-Review Pack #1) pins the proactive rule (volunteer; Standard threshold `extra ≥ 2` or any unprogrammed exercise; silent on `has_extra:false`), the engine-fact ceiling (set counts + names only — no fabricated fatigue/effort), and the D1–D4 wordings. This is the merge gate for the wiring PR's coach tests; docs-only, no production code.
    - ✅ **Wired into the coach voice.** `buildChatContext` (`index.js`) computes `extra_work` for the LIVE session — prescribed = `current_plan` (`sets` = target), logged = `current_preview` grouped to per-exercise set counts (temporally consistent; missing target_sets never guessed; **gated on a real plan — no `current_plan` ⇒ no-extra shape**, so freestyle logging never flags unplanned "extra") — bounded by `sanitizeChatContext` (null when nothing is extra). Coach + route-level (`api-smoke`) tests, incl. the no-plan guard regression; full suite green (2090). **All three approved voice signals (layoff, substitution quality, extra work) now live.**
      - **Deferred (corpus ↔ shipped wording reconciliation)** `[correctness]` — #444's owner-approved corpus (above) specifies a **volunteer** proactive rule (Standard threshold `extra ≥ 2`); the shipped chat prompt (`services/coach.js`) currently words extra work **on-ask/brief** (raised unprompted only when it works against recovery / today's recommendation). Reconcile in the voice follow-up: either bring the prompt to the volunteer/`extra ≥ 2` corpus, or update the corpus to on-ask. Owner decision; no engine/number change either way.
      - **Deferred (PR #440 review)** `[correctness, edge-case]` — extra-work matching is **name-only**: `prescribedForExtra`/logged counts carry no `lift_code`, so a plan/preview name mismatch for the same lift (plan `current_plan.name` = `canonicalName||name`; preview `current_preview.exercise` = raw `activeExercise`) can misreport a planned lift as an `extra_exercise`. `detectExtraWork` already supports `lift_code` matching (identity supersedes name) — harden by threading `lift_code` through `current_plan` + `current_preview` (frontend: `currentPlanForChat`/`currentPreviewRowsForChat` in `public/app.js`) and into `buildChatContext`'s prescribed/logged shapes. Low blast radius (on-ask/recovery-gated wording). Suggested: frontend + `index.js` thread-through; Opus 4.8 (touches `public/app.js`).
  - **Weekly-objective ledger** — track per-week movement-pattern / muscle-volume targets so the voice can say "get the bench day in this week" and judge a reschedule (B16) vs an abandoned objective (B10/B20). Extends `muscleVolume.js` / `underCoverage.js` to a weekly-intent view.
  - ◐ **Return-after-layoff / catch-up guard** — **engine signal built**: `services/layoffGuard.js::assessLayoff(logRows, {today})` returns `{days_since_last_session, returning_from_layoff, severity (none/mild/significant/extended), volume_factor (1.0/0.8/0.66/0.5), watch}`. Pure/deterministic, DST-safe whole-day diff, accepts 12-col rows or `{date_clean|date}` objects. 10 golden tests. Drives B18. **Current-state note:** per-*lift* gap handling already exists (`recommendNextSet`: >10 days → repeat weight; ≥7 → note). The remaining gap was a *session-level* make-up-volume cap; `assessLayoff` now provides that signal.
    - ✅ **Follow-up (wiring) — done:** `scoreIntents` now applies `assessLayoff`'s `volume_factor` to `target_sets` for the four training intents (build_strength / build_muscle / fix_blind_spots / balanced) in a single post-pass before the sort, adds a `returning_from_layoff` reason_code + the ease-back `watch`, and floors sets at 2. Recovery / short_session / test / deload intents (own volume handling) are untouched. Golden fixtures prove layoff → reduced sets + flag and normal cadence → unchanged; full suite green (2078). **Return-after-layoff guard is now live end-to-end.** Coach-voice wording of the layoff `watch` is the later owner-gated step (criterion 4).
  - **Form-breakdown / rep-quality signal** — B14 has **no objective input today** (no velocity/RPE-form capture). Either an opt-in user flag or out of scope until a real signal exists; the voice must NEVER infer form from rep counts alone. Lowest priority / decision needed.
- **Preference learning from edits** (larger build): Atlas learns the user's preferences from how they modify suggested sessions over time (favoured/dropped lifts, variations, edit patterns); biases future suggestions toward what's actually picked; flags novel lifts the user introduces as candidates to cycle in for variety. Shapes selection/rotation only — engine still owns every number.
- **AC8 — phantom-set suppression** (credibility floor for the voice): three rules in the parser / save path: (a) if the message is a question → answer it, log nothing; (b) if the lift name can't be confidently resolved → do NOT save, echo "didn't catch that lift — which one?" and wait; (c) never celebrate a set that wasn't actually logged. Never log-and-flag in the same breath. See [`CONVERSATION_DESIGN.md`](./CONVERSATION_DESIGN.md) AC8.
  - ✅ **Engine slice (deterministic guard, unwired):** `services/messageIntent.js` — pure `classifyMessageIntent(rawText, { hasResolvedLift, hasSetTokens })` → `{ isQuestion, suppressLog, reason }` where `reason` ∈ `question | unresolved_lift | null`. Encodes the floor: a real resolved set always logs (even if it also asks something — the "Crushed 235x8, should I go up?" both-case); a pure question is answered and logs nothing; an unresolved lift is not saved (ask which one). `isQuestionText` is conservative — a bare past-tense log ("did 225x5") is not a question, and openers are `\b`-anchored so they can't fire on lift names ("arnold press" ≠ "are"). 11 golden tests in `test/messageIntent.test.js`, including the documented 2026-06-16 "Didn't you suggest 225 5/2 x3" phantom-set bug. No parser/save-path/LLM edit.
  - ✅ **Wiring — rule (a), the question→phantom bug (owner-scoped):** `parseWorkoutText` (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) now applies `classifyMessageIntent` after `parseLogSets`; when the message reads as a question and did NOT resolve to a real set, it returns `intent:'question'` (`warnings:['logged_nothing_question']`) instead of letting `parseUnknownExercise` fabricate a logged set. The frontend already treats any non-`log_sets` intent as "show message, build no rows" (`rowsFromBackendParsedWorkout`, `public/app.js:2467`), so **no `app.js`/`index.js` edit was needed** — no phantom row reaches the write path. Safety property: a genuinely resolved set (real lift + tokens) is never suppressed, so the both-case ("Bench 235 8/2, should I go up?") still logs. 6 golden tests in `test/parser-golden.test.js` (incl. the exact 2026-06-16 bug). Full suite 2041 pass.
  - **Deferred — wiring rule (b), unresolved-lift refusal** `[correctness]`: a NON-question unrecognised lift with set tokens (`"zercher thrust 95 8/2"`) still logs as `unknown_exercise` / `needsCatalogReview` (the existing new-exercise flow), rather than refusing with "didn't catch that lift — which one?" per AC8 rule (b). The engine already classifies this as `reason:'unresolved_lift'`; wiring it changes the existing catalogue-review logging behavior (could drop a set the user intended to record), so it needs an **owner decision: refuse-and-ask vs keep-flag-and-log**. Separate PR once decided.
  - **Deferred — rule (a) residual: a future-tense question that resolves to a real lift still logs** `[correctness]`: `"Should I bench 225 5/2?"` resolves to Bench Press + set tokens, so the both-case precedence (`hasResolvedLift && hasSetTokens` ⇒ log) logs it despite being a question. The engine can't distinguish a hypothetical ("should I …") from a real-log-plus-question ("crushed 235x8, should I go up?") on lift+tokens alone. A refinement could treat a question opener that PRECEDES the lift/sets (leading question) as hypothetical → suppress, while a trailing question still logs — but that risks the both-case, so it's a deliberate engine change for its own PR, not part of the rule-(a) wiring. Opus 4.8.
  - **Deferred — rule (c), never celebrate a suppressed set** `[polish]`: the `question`/suppressed path shows the parser message but the coach celebration copy lives in `public/coach-conversation.js`; confirm no celebration fires on a non-`log_sets` result (it shouldn't today, since celebration is keyed off a logged set) and add a guard/test if a gap is found. Low priority; Sonnet 4.6.
- **Voice-relevant conversational scenarios** — part of the [`CONVERSATION_DESIGN.md`](./CONVERSATION_DESIGN.md) epic but flagged here as voice-priority work: log-vs-ask intent switching, mid-session stats queries, and conversational undo. These define correct voice behaviour under ambiguous input and must be resolved before or alongside PR 3.4 wording.

## Deterministic Coach Voice Renderer — ◐ slice 1 (pure engine + tests) shipped; wiring deferred

`[correctness]` Atlas currently falls back to generic robotic templates when the LLM is unavailable, rate-limited, or too expensive to call. Known gym events should render deterministically from facts — still human, specific, and coach-like — without requiring a model call. Many set-reaction and next-up responses do not need model reasoning at all. **Principle: the deterministic engine owns facts and decisions; the LLM is optional narration; non-LLM responses should still feel like Atlas, not like system messages.**

**Scope:** create `services/deterministicCoachRenderer.js` — a pure, stateless renderer for these event types:

- `set_logged` — set was recorded cleanly
- `target_hit` — actual result matched the prescribed target
- `reps_missed` — fell short of the rep target
- `rir_too_low` — RIR came in below the prescribed floor (grinding)
- `rir_too_high` — RIR came in above the prescribed ceiling (undershooting)
- `hold_weight` — engine verdict is hold; explain why
- `progress_next_time` — engine verdict is progress; give the exact next load
- `next_up_handoff` — hand off to the next planned exercise
- `substitution_accepted` — swap was classified and accepted
- `skipped_exercise` — exercise removed from the active session
- `inserted_accessory` — exercise added mid-session
- `identity_corrected` — exercise name re-resolved to a canonical
- `warmup_logged` — warm-up set recorded; excluded from progression
- `first_session_back` — returning after a gap; ease-back framing
- `coach_unavailable` — LLM is down; deterministic path is the output, not an apology
- `save_succeeded` — session written to Sheets successfully
- `save_failed` — session write failed; actionable message, not a system dump

**Renderer input shape** (all fields optional/nullable — renderer must never invent a number absent from its input):

```
{ exercise, target: { weight, reps, rir, sets }, actual: { weight, reps, rir },
  previousHistory, trainingGap, progressionStatus, sessionContext,
  mutationContext, readinessContext }
```

**Output shape:** `{ message: string, evidenceCited: boolean }` — short enough for live gym use.

**Acceptance criteria:**

- No generic praise without evidence (facts from input only; no invented numbers).
- Messages short enough for live gym use (1–3 sentences max per event).
- Multiple phrasing variants per event type (minimum 2; chosen by a deterministic hash/index, not random).
- Format: evidence → verdict → next action (mirrors the conclusion-first ordering in `docs/COACHING_NOTE_VOICE.md`).
- LLM outage path (`coach_unavailable` + `coachVoiceTemplates.js` fallbacks) routes to this renderer instead of raw system text.
- Logging, save, and next-up surfaces remain deterministic and do not acquire new LLM dependency.
- Unit tests cover each event type; each test asserts no hallucinated facts (output contains only values present in the input, or stock phrases).

**Example (target_hit event):**
- Before: `"Dialled in — that landed right on target."`
- After: `"Good set. You hit 225×8 at RIR 2, so this is right where we want it. Hold 225 for the next set and keep the reps clean."`

**Priority:** after the current P0 ActiveSession / save / preview-reliability work and coach robustness fixes, but before deep multi-provider LLM routing. This directly improves coach trust and reduces LLM dependency for routine gym events.

**Build order (suggested):** engine + tests first (pure renderer, no route/LLM change) → wire into the coach-unavailable fallback path → wire into set-reaction as a pre-LLM fast path for simple events → voice phrasing pass.

- ✅ **Slice 1 — pure engine + tests (this PR).** `services/deterministicCoachRenderer.js`: `renderCoachEvent(eventType, input)` → `{ message, evidenceCited }` for all 17 event types, each with ≥2 deterministically-chosen phrasing variants (stable djb2 hash, no randomness), conclusion-first, ≤3 sentences. NEVER invents a number — only interpolates values present in the input (or stock phrases); `coach_unavailable` is confident output, not an apology; `save_failed` reassures the sets are kept + is actionable. Pure/stateless, no route/LLM/write change. 15 unit tests in `test/deterministicCoachRenderer.test.js` incl. the hard guarantee (every output digit must appear in the input — full-input subset + empty-input zero-digits), determinism, ≤3-sentence bound, evidenceCited ↔ number-present, variant coverage, and no-throw on malformed input.
- **Remaining slices (deferred, own PRs):** (2) route the `coach_unavailable` / `coachVoiceTemplates.js` LLM-down fallback through this renderer instead of raw template text; (3) wire as a pre-LLM fast path for simple set-reaction events; (4) voice phrasing pass. None acquire a new LLM dependency.

## New-user onboarding + working-weight discovery (B8) — engine layer complete; UX (PR-O4) deferred to owner direction

Owner-approved design pack: [`docs/ONBOARDING_WORKING_WEIGHT_SPEC.md`](./docs/ONBOARDING_WORKING_WEIGHT_SPEC.md) (Owner Review Pack #2, approved 2026-06-20). Not promoted to `docs/ACTIVE_ROADMAP.md` — owner did not reorder roadmap priorities. Build only when promoted. Owner calls locked in the spec's "Owner-approved decisions" section: full-body ×3 default shape; graduation **per-lift at `medium` (3 logged sessions)**, copy-only (never imply fully dialed in); **per-lift** calibration state only (if squat is calibrated and deadlift is unknown, Atlas says so); a user-stated number seeds the start hint but **never** raises confidence (confidence comes only from logged sessions). All numbers trace to existing engine facts (`buildWorkingWeightProtocol` 70% start hint; `exerciseBenchmark` ladder 0=none / 1–2=low / 3–4=medium / 5+=high; `buildWarmupRamp`; `confidence_factors`; `assessLayoff`; `loadSanity`; AC8 phantom-set floor). Deterministic-engine-first, voice-second; tiny PRs. **Approved implementation order:**

- **PR-O1 — onboarding state** `[correctness]` (engine, pure): `services/onboardingState.js` derives a per-lift `calibration_status` (`calibrating` | `graduated`) **purely from the existing `lift_confidence` ladder** — `graduated` at `medium` (≥3 logged sessions). No new data, no schema, no write path, no LLM. Golden fixtures F4/F5 from the spec. Per-lift only (owner call 4).
- ✅ **PR-O2 — onboarding session-template builder** `[correctness]` (engine, pure, shipped) `services/onboardingSessionPlan.js::buildOnboardingSessionPlan({ availableEquipment, lifts })` emits the spec §3 full-body calibration plan (3 sessions, 8 reps @ 2 RIR across squat/push/pull/hinge, widening to a vertical push + one isolation in S2/S3), selecting a lift variant per pattern from available equipment. Numbers are engine-owned: start hints from `buildWorkingWeightProtocol` (70% / "Start conservative"), ramps from `buildWarmupRamp` offered **only once a working weight exists** (F7), per-lift `calibration_status` from PR-O1's `calibrationStatusFor` (no majority gate). Fixtures F1–F3, F7 pinned in `test/onboardingSessionPlan.test.js`. No write path/schema/route/LLM change.
- ✅ **PR-O3 — voice gate** `[polish]` (2026-06-24, reclassified autonomous per Escalation Policy v2 — words an existing engine fact and enforces the existing "never recommend a load for an uncalibrated lift" trust rule; no product-scope/schema/write change): the set-reaction coaching note now gates load presentation on `calibration_status`. `sanitizeFacts` whitelists `calibration_status` to the two onboardingState enum values (`calibrating`/`graduated`; anything else, incl. an unknown confidence or absent → null = no gate) via new `sanitizeCalibrationStatus`, accepted off the facts or the per-lift `rec`. `buildCoachSystemPrompt` gains a rule: for a `calibrating` lift, present any load only as a conservative **start hint**, NEVER a recommendation/verdict/target, never imply "dialed in", frame uncertainty as the process working (never apologize), and a user-stated number stays a start hint (never raises confidence); `graduated`/absent → normal phrasing. Spec §4 (beginner/intermediate/advanced) as the voice reference. Tests in `test/coach.test.js` (whitelist enum + prompt gate). Full suite green (2609).
  - **Deferred (PR-O3 follow-up)** `[polish]`: extend the same gate to the plan "why today" (`buildPlanSystemPrompt`) and the free-form chat (`buildChatSystemPrompt`/`sanitizeChatContext`), and wire the live producer that attaches per-lift `calibration_status` into the set-reaction facts (today the gate is dormant until a caller supplies the field — same defensive "facts may include" pattern as the other engine signals). File the producer wiring with PR-O4 (UX) since both consume onboardingState.
- ✅ **PR-O5 — questionnaire → template assignment router** `[correctness]` (engine, pure, shipped) `services/onboardingRouter.js::routeOnboarding({ goalId, trainingLevel, availableEquipment, daysPerWeek, logEntries, lifts })` routes questionnaire answers to: `cold_start_calibration` (no history → calibration plan), `beginner_lp` (beginner → first recommended starter program), `intermediate_531` (531_wave model → 5/3/1 program id), `advanced_block` (block-intermediate → phase structure), `advanced_dup` (dup-patterns → session types by daysPerWeek). 59 tests. Completes the engine layer for roadmap PR 9.
- **PR-O4 — UX banner** `[polish]` (frontend-only, deferred to owner direction): surface per-lift calibration state to the user (first-run flow / banner). Per-lift, not majority-gated (owner call 4). No write path/schema/LLM change.
- **Deferred (not built, no PR until owner promotes):** pre-session reference / equipment **intake UX** — must honor the Someday "readiness — non-intrusive / never interrogate" guardrail; and **onboarding goal capture** (`getProfileGoal` / goal classifier exist; capturing at onboarding is the deferred piece). File-only here; revisit when onboarding is promoted to the active roadmap.
- **Deferred (PR-O2 #454 review note, for PR-O3/O4 wiring)** `[correctness, edge-case]`: `buildOnboardingSessionPlan`'s `normalizeEquipment` distinguishes `null`/missing `availableEquipment` (→ full-gym default) from an explicit `[]` (→ every pattern `unsupported`, empty slots). That's the correct reading of "no equipment," but when the wiring layer (PR-O3/O4) sources equipment, decide whether an empty/unknown signal should mean "full gym" or "nothing trainable" so a caller passing `[]` to mean "unknown" can't silently produce an empty plan. Module is unwired today, so no behavior change needed now.

**Preconditions (already shipped — no onboarding PR may weaken them):** the AC8 phantom-set floor (`services/messageIntent.js`) and load sanity (`services/loadSanity.js`).

## Settings / user-preferences panel — NEEDS DESIGN (not yet scoped)

Status: parked; needs a design pass before any build.

Confirmed anchor: the chattiness/verbosity slider above.

Starting material to flush out — candidate categories:
- **Voice & personality**: chattiness dial, tone, celebration sensitivity, emoji/formatting.
- **Training defaults**: units, default RIR, goal lifts/targets, progression pace, load rounding.
- **Session shaping**: session size, variety-vs-consistency, lifts to prioritize/avoid, accessory volume.
- **Interaction**: confirmation style (preview-vs-save-and-echo, clarify-vs-infer), optional post-session rating.

Muscles align to `muscleCoverage.js` (17-muscle taxonomy); `movement_pattern` uses the owner's generic enum (NOT the engine's `VALID_PATTERNS`). `exercise_id` is a NEW namespace, distinct from `lift_code` (BEN01…) — the KB never reads/writes `lift_code`. No parser-grammar / schema / write-path / trust-loop change. (Update: the Cable Fly fix added the first **read-only** live use — a parse-route identity lookup to suppress a false warning; the KB still does not touch the write/coaching/lift_code paths.)

Do not build identity infrastructure before KB shape is settled.** (owner-gated — touches write-contract identity); (4) feed KB `movement_pattern`/muscles/`exercise_type` into the substitution/fatigue/role engines via the documented KB→engine pattern map, replacing the regex heuristics incrementally.

Extend the enum or derive isolation pattern from `substitution_group`+`primary_muscles` if precise isolation patterns matter for coaching.

- ◐ **Post-log next-up wrong at session end after a swap-then-cancel (diagnostic added; root cause needs runtime state)** `[correctness]` (owner retest 2026-06-22, **confirmed shell v23**, backend up) — *#484 (diagnostic); root cause found + fixed above.* On confirmed-current code the core fixes WORK: `Weighted dips`→planned `Weighted Dip` (handoff advanced to Lat Pulldown), `Incline`→`Incline DB Press` (shorthand re-parse), effort notes (RIR-0 block / high-RIR bump), and mid-session handoffs all correct. **Two reported symptoms are actually ONE bug:** the composer placeholder is set at `coach-conversation.js:1066` from the **same `nextEx`** the handoff uses, so the "stuck on Weighted Dip" composer just mirrors a wrong **end-of-session** handoff — after the LAST lift (Face Pull) next-up resolved to the already-completed **Weighted Dip** instead of "session complete." **Trigger:** the run included a **swap-then-cancel** detour ("Lat bar taken, I'll do seated rows instead" → "Nvm lat bar is back"), which mutates `activePlannedSession.exercises` via `applySessionSubstitution`'s splice (`app.js:1214`). **Why no blind fix:** the static code can't produce the observed sequence (every mid-session handoff correct, only the final one wrong) under any consistent `sessionCompleted` — so runtime state diverges from the apparent logic. After two prior fixes that looked right but regressed, shipping a third guess into the trust-sensitive substitution path is wrong. **This PR adds a read-only "Show session state" debug dump** (Settings → Debug) exposing `plannedExerciseOrder` / `sessionCompleted` / `remainingPlannedExercises` / `firstUnloggedPlannedLift` / `activePlannedSession` so the next occurrence is diagnosable from real values. SW shell v23→v24. No behavior/parse/write/trust-loop change.
  - **Next:** owner reruns the 6-lift sequence **straight (no swap detour)** to isolate — if clean, the swap-then-cancel is confirmed the trigger and the fix is scoped to `applySessionSubstitution` + the next-up/closeout after a mid-session swap; the "Show session state" dump pins the exact divergence either way. Then ship the precise fix.
  - **Still queued:** offline shorthand re-parse via the local parser (gym wifi); composer↔handoff are already one source (no separate composer fix needed once next-up is correct).

- ◐ **Shell build self-ID (end the "is the new JS actually running?" ambiguity)** `[housekeeping/diagnostic]` (owner retest 2026-06-22, build #481 · 3fc5153) — *#482/#483.* The Settings build badge fetches `/version`, which is **server-sourced** — it confirms Render deployed a commit but NOT that the browser is running that commit's `app.js` (the PWA service worker can serve a **cached older shell**). After #481 a live retest still failed, but it was inconclusive: the badge showed #481 (server) while the running shell may have been cached, AND the backend was degraded during the test (`Coach is unavailable` ×2, which alone disables the shorthand re-parse). Fix: bake `ATLAS_SHELL_BUILD` into `app.js` (mirrors the SW cache name) and show it next to `/version` ("… · shell v22"); a unit test pins `ATLAS_SHELL_BUILD === sw.js` cache version. Now a stale shell is a glance (server build vs shell tag), and future retests are conclusive. SW cache v21→v22. No behavior/parse/write change.
  - ◐ **Deferred (real gap exposed by the outage — `[correctness]`) — partially closed:** a *named* lift the local parser can resolve ("Lat Pulldown 175lbs 8/2…") now falls back to the local parser on a flaky/partial backend response (the no-write-proof / empty-rows fallback shipped above, this PR). **Still open:** the shorthand planned-lead re-parse (`rowsFromUnresolvedPlannedLead`) needs the **backend** parser and is gated to `!shouldUseLocalFallback`, so when the backend is **down/flaky** (gym wifi) a *shorthand*-named lift ("Row", "Incline") still can't attach and falls to chat. Make the planned-lead substitution also run through the **local** parser in the offline fallback (instant, no timeout) so shorthand logs even offline. Extract a pure `rebuildWithPlannedLead(text)` shared by both paths.

- ◐ **Post-log LIVE-path fix (the #480 fix never engaged in the browser)** `[correctness]` (owner live retest — 2026-06-22, build #480 · a15d5b2) — *#481.* #480's helper tests passed but the deployed flow still failed: the reroute deferred a just-completed Weighted Dip, the composer stayed stuck on Weighted Dip, and "Lat pull"/"Incline" silently routed to chat. **Root causes the helper tests missed:** (1) **enrichment/lift_code is only fetched when `activePlannedSession` exists** (started flow) — never in the coach-suggestion flow the owner used — so `resolveCompletedIdentity` had no lift_code, and the logged catalog canonical (`"Dips (Weighted)"`) can't bridge to the planned `"Weighted Dip"` by name (word order differs), leaving the dip in `remainingPlannedExercises`; (2) the parser can't resolve shorthand lift names (`"Lat pull"`, `"Incline"`) → no rows → chat ("Noted — keep logging…"), so those lifts never logged or advanced the composer. **Fix (frontend-only; no parser grammar / schema / write / trust-loop change; routing suggestion-only):** (a) bridge completion by `lift_code` resolved from the **loaded catalog datalist** (`liftCodeFromCatalog`: option value=canonical_name → label=lift_code) inside `resolveCompletedIdentity`, so a logged catalog canonical (`"Dips (Weighted)"`) maps to the planned `"Weighted Dip"` by code in BOTH the started and coach-suggested flows — **with no network call**; (b) new `rowsFromUnresolvedPlannedLead` — when a shorthand-named log's lead alias-matches the pending planned lift, re-parse with the planned lift's full name substituted (reuses the real backend parser; gated to `!shouldUseLocalFallback` so it never adds an offline re-parse timeout, and so an unrelated lift never mis-attaches). SW shell cache v20→v21. **Tests: integration-style** — drive the REAL `emitSetLogged` → `atlas:set-logged` event: datalist bridges the alias with no enrichment/network; no-bridge control reproduces the regression; plus the re-parse helper across shorthand/unrelated/no-plan cases (`test/postLogIdentity.test.js`). Full suite green (2298). **Acceptance 1–7 covered; deferred items below.**
  - **Why the first attempt (a `hasSuggestedPlan` enrichment fetch) was wrong:** it issued a `test_mode` `/api/log-workout` dry-run per conversational set in the suggestion flow, which the E2E guardrails (`previewRequests === 0` during mid-session logging) correctly flagged — mid-session logging must hit no preview/write endpoint. The datalist bridge gets the same `lift_code` with zero network calls, preserving that invariant.
  - **Deferred (polish):** the composer placeholder full-prescription hint still depends on `/api/plan/today` having an exact key for the next lift (else it shows the bare name). Reroute remains suggestion-only and does not move the composer to the suggested pull (strict "no auto-reorder").

- ◐ **Post-log UI state consistency after PR #479 (alias/identity)** `[correctness]` (owner live test — 2026-06-22, build #479 · 4e3a48a) — *this PR.* Live test passed the set-effort engine but exposed a broader post-log state bug: a logged lift whose display/canonical name differs from the plan name (planned **"Weighted Dip"** vs logged **"Dips (Weighted)" / "Weighted dips"**; planned **"Lat Pulldown"** vs shorthand **"Lat pull"**) was not recognized as the planned lift, so `sessionCompleted` / `plannedQueue` / `nextPlanned` / handoff / composer / reroute disagreed — the reroute even told the lifter to "come back to Weighted Dip" *after* it was logged, and the confirmation/next card went missing for shorthand. Root causes: (1) `resolveCompletedIdentity` only matched against `activePlannedSession` (null in the coach-suggestion flow) and required a lift_code/canonical hit — so an alias fell through to the raw name; (2) `plannedQueue` and `nextPlanned` used separate raw-string filters; (3) `liftCodeForExercise` was exact-match only, so a shorthand never resolved a recommendation card. Fix (`public/app.js`, `public/coach-conversation.js`; read-only narration — no write/schema/parser/trust-loop change, routing stays suggestion-only): new `plannedExerciseEntries()` (name+canonical+liftCode, from started **OR** suggested plan) and one shared `remainingPlannedExercises()` that both `nextPlanned` and `plannedQueue` derive from; `resolveCompletedIdentity` generalized to both plan sources with a lift_code → canonical → **alias/contains** ladder; `liftCodeForExercise` gains an alias/contains fallback so the next-prescription card renders for shorthand. SW shell cache v19→v20. Tests: `test/postLogIdentity.test.js` (extracted helpers: alias resolution in both flows, remaining excludes completed, reroute never defers a completed lift, shorthand card resolves) + `unit.test.js`/`sessionPlanExecutor.test.js` source-introspection. Full suite green (2293).
  - **Deferred (polish):** the composer placeholder falls back to the bare next-exercise name when `/api/plan/today` has no exact key for it (loses the full prescription hint) — add a contains lookup in `getPlanTodayByName` consumers if live use wants the richer hint. `planStepFor` ("X of N") still reads only `activePlannedSession`, so the readback step label is absent in the pure coach-suggestion flow (pre-existing; not a regression).
  - **Deferred (review note, #480, non-blocking):** the last-resort `n.includes(rk) || rk.includes(n)` contains-match in `resolveCompletedIdentity` (and the `liftCodeForExercise` fallback) has no word/length-boundary guard, so a very generic logged token could contains-match an unrelated planned name in read-only completion narration (never a Sheet write). **Do not naively add a word-boundary guard** — the substring match is what resolves the intended aliases (`"lat pull"` ⊂ `"lat pulldown"`, `"weighted dips"` ⊃ `"weighted dip"`); a whole-word guard would regress those. If false matches surface live, scope the tightening to length/prefix heuristics that still admit the compound-word aliases, and apply it consistently with `planStepFor`/`getNextExerciseInPlan`.

- ✅ **Composer: compact full-prescription placeholder + bare-set attaches to the planned lift** `[correctness/ux]` (owner — 2026-06-21) — Two live failures with a planned workout open. **(1) Placeholder too thin:** the composer hint showed only the lead set ("Bench Press 230×5"). Fix: `buildWorkoutPlaceholder` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) now renders the compact full prescription via new `compactPrescription(raw)` — short parse-safe lift alias + the engine's warm-up ramp marked `wu` + working set in Atlas shorthand with repeated sets collapsed `xN`, e.g. **"Bench 140x15wu 190x10wu 230 5/2 x3"**. Warm-ups are read off the RAW intent exercise (`warmupSetsFor`, `normalizePlanExercise` drops them) and are **display-only**: the placeholder is the `.placeholder` attribute (never the value), and the parser ignores a `{w}x{r}wu` token, so submitting the hint logs **only the working sets** — never a save-ready warm-up. Aliases (`Bench, Squat, DL, RDL, OHP, Dip`) each round-trip through the canonicalizer (test-asserted); ambiguous shorts (`Row, LatPD, Incline`) are omitted (they don't canonicalize) and keep full names. Full names unchanged in the workout DISPLAY. **(2) Bare set sequence didn't attach:** with the plan open, "140 15 190 10 230 4/2 4/1 3/1" returned "Noted — keep logging…" instead of attaching to Bench. Root cause: the parser attaches bare sets to `context.activeExercise`, which is `null` before the first set is logged. Fix: new `firstUnloggedPlannedLift()` (`public/app.js`, also now the source for `emitSetLogged`'s next-up) is passed as the parse-context `activeExercise` fallback (`activeExercise || firstUnloggedPlannedLift()`), so a bare sequence lands on the current planned lift; with no plan context it still asks to clarify and never coins a phantom lift. No parser change; backend already forwards `context` to the parser; still `test_mode:true` dry-run (preview/approval unchanged). SW shell cache v16→v17. Tests: `test/coachConversation.test.js` (alias round-trip, placeholder format, app wiring), `test/parser-golden.test.js` (bare-set attach, clarify-without-context, placeholder submit logs only working sets). Full suite green (2255). No write-path / schema / proof-field / approval change.
  - ✅ **Parser: bare "weight reps" pairs in a mixed sequence** `[correctness, owner-gated parser]` (owner-approved 2026-06-21) — "140 15 190 10 230 4/2 4/1 3/1" now parses to all **5** sets (140×15, 190×10, 230×4@2, 230×4@1, 230×3@1), incl. with the planned-lift fallback when the name is omitted. Root cause: in `parseDaleShorthand` (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) the single-set `appStyle` regex matched a `tokens.slice(i)` **sub-window** whenever `sets.length===0`, so "140 15 190 10" mis-matched "15 190 10" → `15×190 @10`, and bare `{weight} {reps}` pairs weren't paired in a sequence. Fix (focused; slash-notation contract otherwise unchanged): (1) the single space-separated `{weight} {reps} {rir?}` rule now matches the **whole token string at i===0 only** (so "225 5 2"/"185 8" still parse, and a longer sequence falls through instead of mis-segmenting a sub-window); (2) a new bare-pair branch pairs a plain weight with a following plain integer (`{weight} {reps}`, no RIR) inside a multi-set sequence, setting `currentWeight` so subsequent slash sets attach. Slash-only, `xN` repeats, single-set, and prose-rejection all preserved (golden tests in `test/parser-golden.test.js`). Full suite green (2262). No write-path / schema / proof-field / approval / coach change.
  - **Deferred — deterministic "that was for X" correction** `[polish]`: a follow-up like "that was for bench" still routes to the coach chat (LLM), so a Gemini outage → "Coach is unavailable". With bare-set attachment fixed, the sets land on the planned lift up front, so this correction is rarely needed; a deterministic handler (buffer the last clarification-pending bare sets; on "that was for X"/bare lift name, re-parse them with X) would close the no-active-plan case without the LLM. File when wanted.

- ✅ **Session next-up cursor + feedback-vs-log classification** `[correctness/trust]` (owner — 2026-06-21) — Live workout test surfaced two bugs in the conversational logging flow. **(1) Wrong next-up:** after logging Bench (first in plan Bench → Seated Row → … → Face Pull), Atlas said "next up: Face Pull"; after Seated Row it said "next up: Bench Press". Root cause: `getNextExerciseInPlan` (`public/coach-conversation.js`) used the **API-plan map order** (≠ visible session order) and the `idx+1`-of-just-logged entry, never skipping already-logged lifts. Fix: `app.js::emitSetLogged` now computes `nextPlanned` = the first `activePlannedSession` exercise (visible order) not in `sessionCompleted` and passes it in the read-only `atlas:set-logged` detail; `coach-conversation.js` uses `detail.nextPlanned` for the handoff (API-plan lookup kept only as the freeform fallback). Mirrored by the new pure `nextRemainingExercise(planned, completed)` in `services/sessionPlanExecutor.js` (= `computePlanState().remaining[0]`), unit-tested for in-order, out-of-order, and lift_code-match cases. **(2) Feedback parsed as a log:** "I don't want to do 11 reps on a workout like seated rows 10/2…" was coined into a phantom exercise "I Don't Want To Do" (0×10). Root cause: `extractUnknownExerciseLead` (`services/workoutTextParser.js`) took all words before the first set-token as the lift name. Fix: a `looksLikeProse` guard rejects leads containing conversational words (pronouns/negations/verbs) or running > 4 words → the parser returns no log rows (→ routed to the coach as a question, app.js:3382), never a phantom lift. **No write was ever at risk** — the parse preview is always `test_mode:true` dry-run; this was a false-preview/UX-trust bug, not a false write. The next-up handoff is deterministic (engine/app, no LLM) so it's correct regardless of coach availability. SW shell cache v15→v16. Tests: `test/sessionPlanExecutor.test.js` (next-up + frontend-wiring introspection), `test/parser-golden.test.js` (feedback rejected; genuine unknown lifts + known logs still parse), `test/unit.test.js` (SW bump). Full suite green. No write-path / schema / proof-field / approval change; the slash-notation contract for valid logs is unchanged.
  - **Owner progression preference (capture)** `[engine, deferred]`: for rows / accessory-ish strength work, prefer **proving a standard target (10 reps @ RIR 2 across all sets) before adding load**, rather than prescribing odd targets like 11 reps as the main goal. Encode in the progression engine's rep-target selection for that lift class when the owner wants it wired; for now this is the recorded preference + a future test fixture.
  - **Deferred — deterministic next-up *correction* handler** `[polish]`: when the lifter corrects the order ("X isn't next, Y is"), it currently routes to the coach chat (so a Gemini outage → "Coach is unavailable"). With the deterministic next-up fix the handoff is already correct, so corrections should be rare; but a dedicated deterministic handler (like `buildSessionCloseAnswer` for "are we done?") that re-points the cursor without the LLM would close the gap. File when needed.

- ✅ **Coach chat: named-lift session questions answer from the current plan first** `[correctness]` (owner — 2026-06-21) — Live evidence: with Bench Press 230×5 @ RIR 2 on screen, "what's the RIR for bench?" returned generic RIR/RPE *education* and "how many reps / how much weight for bench?" answered from past *history*, instead of from today's plan. Two diversion points: (1) the frontend SME gate — `public/sessionQuestion.js::isSessionStateQuestion` doesn't match "rir for bench?", so the question leaked to `/api/coach/ask` (training-knowledge SME) before the plan was consulted; (2) the backend chat route — the deterministic plan answer (`buildSessionQuestionAnswer`) only ran as a *Gemini-failure fallback*, so with Gemini up the LLM narrated from history. Fix (engine/session facts only, read-only, no invented numbers): new `answerPlannedLiftQuestion(message, clientContext)` in `services/sessionQuestionAnswer.js` answers a session value (rir/reps/weight/sets) **from the live plan/preview** when the lifter NAMES a lift that is in today's plan — wired into `POST /api/coach/chat` (`index.js`) **before** the Gemini call. It defers (null) for past-tense ("…last time?" → history owns it), unnamed-lift ("what is RIR?" → education, no plan[0] fallback), and off-plan lifts (→ clarify/history). New `public/sessionQuestion.js::isPlannedLiftQuestion(message, liftNames)` lets the frontend gate (`public/coach-conversation.js`) skip the SME for named-planned-lift value questions so they reach the session-aware coach. SW shell cache v14→v15. Tests: `test/sessionQuestionAnswer.test.js` (plan RIR/reps/weight; education/history/off-plan defer) + `test/sessionQuestion.test.js` (classifier + routing). Full suite green (2228). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser change; no new LLM call (the plan answer is deterministic).
  - **Deferred — pass plan context to the SME endpoint** `[polish]`: `/api/coach/ask` still receives only `{ message }`, so an oddly-phrased planned-lift question the frontend classifier misses could still reach the SME and get education. The frontend skip now covers the common framings; if a phrasing leaks live, either widen `isPlannedLiftQuestion` or have `/api/coach/ask` defer when the named lift is in the plan.

- ✅ **Recovery-aware movement density in Build Strength (read ↔ prescription consistency)** `[correctness]` (owner — 2026-06-21) — Live evidence: a Build Strength plan said "Push is still recovering" yet prescribed three pressing movements (Bench Press, Weighted Dip, Incline DB Press). Root cause: `build_strength` selects via `exForPatterns(['push','pull',…])` (filter-by-pattern + recency slice) which never consults per-pattern readiness; the readiness model *did* mark push `recovering`, but that status only fed the intent **score** (`isFatigued`) and **dose** (`applyReadinessDose`) — and both fire only on `'fatigued'`, neither on `'recovering'`, and neither reduces movement **density**. Fix (engine-only, drop-only, reuses existing readiness metadata — no new recovery rule): new `capRecoveringPatternDensity(exercises)` in `services/analytics.js::scoreIntents` thins any pattern whose `rm[pattern].status` is `'recovering'` or `'fatigued'` down to its single highest-priority movement (`main > secondary > accessory` via `classifyLiftRole`, ties by recency) and reports which patterns it trimmed; `build_strength` runs it before `structureSession` (so #466's `capSessionToProfile` is unchanged) and adds a `why_today` line + `recovering_pattern_density_capped` reason code so the read and the prescription agree. Tests in `test/analytics-edge.test.js` (recovering push → one press, the main, pull retained, why text + code; fresh push → all three retained). Full suite green (2214). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
  - ✅ **Extended recovery-aware density to the `buildIntentSession` intents** `[correctness]` (2026-06-24) — `build_muscle` / `fix_blind_spots` / `balanced` now run `capRecoveringPatternDensity` before `structureSession`, at the **higher hypertrophy cap of 2 movements** per recovering/fatigued pattern (vs `build_strength`'s 1) — hypertrophy tolerates more volume on a recovering pattern than a strength day. `capRecoveringPatternDensity(exercises, cap=1)` is now parameterized (`build_strength` byte-identical at the default 1); the three intents pass `HYPERTROPHY_RECOVERING_CAP=2`, surface the trim in `why_today` (shared `recoveringTrimWhy` helper) and carry the `recovering_pattern_density_capped` reason code. Tests in `test/analytics-edge.test.js` (recovering push → 2 presses incl. the main, pull retained, why + code; balanced same cap + why; fresh push → all 3 retained). Full suite green. No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
    - **Deferred (observation, PR #543 review)** `[polish]`: unlike `build_strength`, the hypertrophy intents don't run `backfillToProfileTarget` after the trim, so a recovering-pattern cap could leave the session below the learned session norm. The higher cap of 2 mitigates this and these intents draw from broader pattern lists, so it's low-risk — wire the backfill into them only if a capped hypertrophy day reads as too short in live use.

- **STRATEGIC DIRECTION (owner — 2026-06-21) — data-driven session design for data-rich users.** Owner has ~a year of logged sessions; Atlas should compose workouts from how he *actually* trains, not generic templates. Guiding principle (same as the personalized ramp): **learn the structure from the lifter's data; let the engine own the smart numbers** — NOT a pure replay of history (that stops it coaching). Learn session *shapes*, exercise *vocabulary/rotation*, *volume norms*, and *pairings* from logs → then layer progression, recovery, the ramp, and the occasional blind-spot *within* that learned structure. The "ask the user / generic caps" path is the **sparse-data fallback** (new users), not the default for data-rich users. Roadmap (slices):
  - ✅ **(1) session-volume profile engine** — `services/sessionVolumeProfile.js::buildSessionVolumeProfile(logRows, { today, windowDays=120, minSessions=6 })` returns the lifter's own `{ exercises_per_session, compounds_per_session, accessories_per_session, session_cap (75th pct), sessions_analyzed, confidence }` (median distinct exercises/session split by `classifyLiftRole`), or `null` below `minSessions` (→ keep generic caps). Pure, read-only, unwired. Motivated by a live "Build Strength" day showing 1 compound + 5 accessories (felt bloated). Golden tests in `test/sessionVolumeProfile.test.js`.
  - ✅ **(2) wire the calibration** — `services/sessionBuilder.js::capSessionToProfile(exercises, profile)` sizes the suggested session to the lifter's **own learned norm** (the all-body, per-user analog of `capLowerBodyAccessoriesForHeavyLegDay`). All caps are **data-derived from the profile output, not constants**: `buildSessionVolumeProfile` now also emits `accessory_cap` (75th-pct of per-session accessory count); the trimmer reads `profile.session_cap` (total) and `profile.accessory_cap` and is **drop-only** — it trims **accessories above the accessory cap first**, then **secondary** lifts only if still over the total cap, and **never drops a main compound**. No profile / insufficient history (`null`) → no trim (generic engine behavior, the sparse-data fallback). Wired as the final step of `structureSession` (the shared pass for the 4 main training intents) in `services/sessionBuilder.js`; `scoreIntents` (`services/analytics.js`) builds the profile once via `buildSessionVolumeProfile(logRows, { today })` and threads it through `structureOpts.sessionProfile`. Validated against the owner's **real** `Log_Cleaned` (92 sessions; median 5 ex/session; `session_cap` 6; `accessory_cap` 2; compound-dominant) — normal 4–6-exercise days are untouched; only 7–8-exercise outliers trim. Explainability tests prove the cap follows the profile (cap 4 vs 6 → different results; accessory_cap 2 vs 1 → different results; mains never dropped; object-row reader) in `test/session-builder.test.js` + `test/sessionVolumeProfile.test.js`. No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
    - **Deferred — read-only profile surface** `[polish]`: the owner can't yet *see* their session-volume profile in-app (it's engine-internal). Surface `{ exercises_per_session, compounds_per_session, accessories_per_session, session_cap, accessory_cap, confidence }` read-only (e.g. a diagnostics/Settings line or a `/version`-style endpoint) so the learned norm is visible. File when the owner wants the visibility.
    - **Watch — `fix_blind_spots` trailing-accessory trim** `[polish]` (PR #466 Atlas review, non-blocking): `capSessionToProfile` drops *trailing* accessories by role order, so on an over-stuffed `fix_blind_spots` day a neglected-pattern lift that happens to land as a trailing accessory could be trimmed. Consistent with "size to the learned norm," but if a blind-spot day ever reads as under-targeted, teach the trimmer to protect the credited blind-spot lift (skip it when choosing what to drop). No action unless that surfaces live.
  - **(3+) later slices**: learned session **archetypes** (real upper/lower/pull days), exercise **vocabulary + rotation**, typical **pairings** — all data-derived, generic engine as the sparse-data fallback. Medium-high risk (prescription composition); owner-directed. Opus 4.8.
- ✅ **`fix_blind_spots` gated on a consistent-training baseline (a layoff is not a blind-spot day)** `[correctness]` (owner insight — 2026-06-21) — Live evidence: after the owner missed ~9 days, the recommended session was `fix_blind_spots` ("attacking your lower body and hinge patterns, both overdue after 9 days"). Root cause confirmed by repro: `fix_blind_spots` scored `40 + 20×(fresh/overdue patterns)`, and a layoff makes **every** pattern "fresh" → score explodes (120+) → it wins. Owner's point: a blind spot is a pattern neglected *relative to consistent training* ("crashing the gym week after week, hitting all the major groups"), not the uniform "everything's overdue" of a layoff / all-green-fatigue slow week. **Fix (deterministic, engine-only):** the `+20`-per-overdue-pattern bonus now applies only when `trainingConsistently = fatigue.status !== 'low' && readiness.some(status ∈ {fatigued, recovering, ready})` — i.e. overall load isn't low AND at least one pattern was trained recently (contrast). On a uniform layoff the bonus is withheld, `fix_blind_spots` stays at base, and the general training intents win (eased-back via the existing `assessLayoff` volume_factor) instead of chasing "overdue" patterns. Repro verified: layoff → `build_strength` (fbs 120→60); consistent-training-with-neglected-core still credits fbs (>60). Tests in `test/analytics-edge.test.js` (layoff-not-blind-spots + consistent-still-credits). Full suite green (2176). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
  - **Deferred — explicit "return / build-momentum" framing + relative-cadence blind spots** `[polish]`: today a suppressed-blind-spot layoff routes to the general training intents (correct session, eased-back volume), but there's no dedicated "welcome back / rebuild" *label/copy*, and a blind spot is still measured in absolute days-since (`fresh`) rather than relative to the lifter's own normal cadence per pattern. Both are refinements on top of the shipped gate; file when the owner wants the framing.
- ✅ **Major-lift warm-up ramp surfaced in the live plan display** `[correctness]` — Live owner evidence (2026-06): major compounds (Deadlift, Back Squat, Overhead Press) displayed flat repeated working sets ("245 7/2 ×3") with no ramp into the working weight — the exact "flat sets from set one" bug named in `SESSION_DESIGN.md` "Set progression — warm-up ramps". Root cause was two-fold: (1) the **`build_strength` intent** built its exercises via `exForPatterns`, which — unlike `buildIntentSession` — attached no warm-up ramp to the lead compound; (2) the **live suggested-workout display** (`public/coach-conversation.js` `appendWorkoutPlan` / `suggestedExercisesBlock`) dropped the engine's `warmup_sets` entirely (and `normalizePlanExercise` strips them). Fix (engine numbers unchanged — reuses the existing `buildWarmupRamp` 50/70/85% policy): new pure `attachAnchorWarmup(exercises)` in `services/sessionBuilder.js` ramps the lead compound (first non-LOW-cost lift) of an ordered list — idempotent (skips lists `buildIntentSession` already ramped), no ramp when the working weight is unknown (never fabricates a load), accessories/isolations stay flat. Wired into `build_strength` only (`services/analytics.js`, before the readiness dose). Display renders `warmup_sets` as dimmed, RIR-less "· warm-up" priming lines **before** the working sets in both render paths (new `.workout-plan-warmup` class). Planned warm-ups are narration only — they never reach `startLift`/the logger, so planned-vs-completed stays separate and no save-ready rows are generated. Tests: `attachAnchorWarmup` unit suite (`test/session-builder.test.js` — Deadlift/Squat/OHP ramp, accessory flat, no-weight no-fabrication, lead-only, idempotent), a live `scoreIntents` integration test (`test/analytics-edge.test.js` — build_strength lead compound ascends into the working weight; exactly one ramp; accessory flat), and a display source-introspection test (`test/unit.test.js`). Full suite green (2152). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
  - **Deferred — ramp the lead compound on the other exForPatterns intents** `[correctness]`: `recovery_pump`, `short_session`, and `test_progress` also build via `exForPatterns` and so still show a flat lead compound. `recovery_pump` deliberately remaps load to 0.75× working weight **after** `exForPatterns` returns, so it must **not** ramp (a ramp computed off the un-reduced weight would mismatch the light working set) — leave flat. `short_session` / `test_progress` arguably *should* ramp the lead compound (heavy work, especially a test attempt), but applying it needs per-intent handling to avoid the recovery pitfall. Out of scope for the one-concern PR. **Suggested PR type:** per-intent `attachAnchorWarmup` wiring (skip recovery) + tests; Opus 4.8.

It renders a single `weight × reps × sets` target line per exercise (not repeated per-set rows), so the "flat sets from set one" visual bug does **not** manifest there the same way, but the lead-compound ramp won't show there either. `public/app.js` is a restricted trust-loop file, so it was correctly left out of this one-concern PR. Fold in for display parity if the owner wants the ramp visible on the drawer surface too. **Suggested PR type:** frontend `public/app.js` drawer-render edit (name app.js in scope) + test; Opus 4.8. (Raised in the PR #456 automated review.)

File-only; revisit if a second cold heavy compound needs its own ramp.
- ✅ **Strength session: role ordering + per-pattern major-compound ramps** `[correctness]` — Follow-up to the ramp work above. Live owner evidence: after #456 the lead compound (Deadlift) ramped, but **Back Squat appeared flat and *after* Leg Extension / Leg Curl**. Root cause (one shared origin): the `build_strength` intent assembles via `exForPatterns`, which is in **recency order** (`allRecs` sorted by last-trained) with no role ordering, and #456's `attachAnchorWarmup` ramped only the **first non-LOW-cost** lift — so a second main compound of a different pattern stayed flat and accessories could sit between compounds. Fix (engine numbers unchanged): replaced `attachAnchorWarmup` with two pure helpers in `services/sessionBuilder.js` — `orderByRole(exercises)` (stable sort main→secondary→accessory via the existing `classifyLiftRole`, name-first) and `attachMainCompoundWarmups(exercises)` (ramp the **first main compound of each movement pattern** via `patternFor`; same-pattern later main stays flat; secondary/accessory never ramp; idempotent; no ramp without a known working weight). Wired into `build_strength` only: `applyReadinessDose(attachMainCompoundWarmups(orderByRole(exForPatterns(strengthPatterns))))`. Live result: Deadlift (hinge) + Back Squat (squat) both ramp; leg press (secondary) then leg ext/curl (accessory) ordered last and flat. Tests: `orderByRole` + `attachMainCompoundWarmups` unit suites (`test/session-builder.test.js`) and an updated live `scoreIntents` integration test (`test/analytics-edge.test.js`). Full suite green. No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change. recovery_pump / short_session / test_progress untouched.
  - **Deferred — reduced-depth "already-warm" priming** `[polish]`: a second main compound sharing an already-warm pattern (e.g. RDL after Deadlift — both hinge) now stays **flat**. `SESSION_DESIGN.md` permits a *reduced* ramp ("minimal or none") there; "none" is honored today. A reduced 1–2-step priming sequence needs a `buildWarmupRamp(weight, { depth })` option that does not yet exist — build it before claiming reduced-depth priming rather than fabricating a partial ramp. (Supersedes the older "anchor-only approximation" note above for the `build_strength` path.)

Apply the same `orderByRole` + `attachMainCompoundWarmups` treatment there once the `build_strength` change is validated live. Out of scope for this one-concern PR.

The reported burying bug is fixed without this; a deliberate primary/secondary ranking (by goal/pattern priority or e1RM trend) is a separate enhancement — file an owner decision before encoding one.
- ✅ **Strength session: lower-body volume budget (double-heavy-leg-day cap)** `[correctness]` — Live owner evidence: a strength session stacked Deadlift + Back Squat + single-leg leg press + leg extension + leg curl — five lower-body stressors, too much for a normal strength day. Root cause: `build_strength` had **no session-level volume/fatigue cap** — `exForPatterns` took the top lifts by recency. Fix (deterministic, drop-only — never reweights or fabricates): new pure `capLowerBodyAccessoriesForHeavyLegDay(exercises, { maxLowerAccessories = 1 })` in `services/sessionBuilder.js` — when ≥2 **main lower-body compounds** are present (squat/hinge patterns via `patternFor` + `classifyLiftRole`), it caps **lower-body accessories** (knee/calf/hip isolation) to 1, dropping the surplus. Mains, secondary compounds (leg press), and ALL upper-body work are untouched; fires only at the double-heavy threshold so a normal single-compound leg day is unchanged. Wired into `build_strength` only, after role ordering/ramps, before the readiness dose: `applyReadinessDose(capLowerBodyAccessoriesForHeavyLegDay(attachMainCompoundWarmups(orderByRole(exForPatterns(...)))))`. `recovery_pump` / `short_session` / `test_progress` untouched. Tests: helper unit suite + live `scoreIntents` integration (`test/analytics-edge.test.js`). Full suite green (2160). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
  - **Deferred — tune the cap + secondary-compound reduction** `[polish]`: the owner's direction allowed "reduce accessory count **or** reduce secondary compound demand." The shipped rule reduces accessory count (cap = 1 lower-body accessory) and leaves the secondary leg-press compound and main set counts intact, so a double-heavy day still totals 2 mains + 1 secondary + 1 accessory. If live testing still reads as too much, options to file as a follow-up: cap to 0 lower accessories, or trim the secondary compound's sets, or treat one of two heavy compounds as a reduced/technique lift. Conservative starting threshold chosen deliberately; revisit with owner feedback.

Fix: new shared `structureSession(exercises)` in `services/sessionBuilder.js` = strip any builder-applied ramp/anchor → `orderByRole` → `attachMainCompoundWarmups` → `capLowerBodyAccessoriesForHeavyLegDay`; wired into all four training intents (`build_strength`, `build_muscle`, `fix_blind_spots`, `balanced`) before the readiness dose. `build_strength` output is unchanged (exForPatterns carries no prior ramp, so the strip is a no-op). Also corrects the secondary-anchor-ramp bug (a Leg Press anchor no longer ramps). `recovery_pump` / `short_session` / `test_progress` (their own exForPatterns lists) untouched. Tests: `structureSession` unit suite + live `scoreIntents` integration for the hypertrophy/general recommended intents (`test/analytics-edge.test.js`). Full suite green (2167). No write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.

Invariant guard test in `test/analytics-edge.test.js` (every overdue pattern surfaced in the brief has a same-pattern exercise in the final session). Full suite green (2600). So `orderByRole` / `attachMainCompoundWarmups` / `capLowerBodyAccessoriesForHeavyLegDay` get `classifyLiftRole(name, muscle_group)` and the `ACCESSORY_MUSCLES` fallback fires — a keyword-less accessory (e.g. "Pallof Press" / Core, which matches no name pattern) now reads as `accessory` instead of `secondary`, so it sorts after the secondary lifts and is subject to the lower-body cap rather than sitting among the compounds. Completes the muscle_group threading across the engine's session consumers. Pure engine; no write-path / schema / proof-field / parser / LLM change.
